*Stefanie Rinderle-Ma,Juergen Mangler,Johannes Loebbecke,Dominik Voigt,Nataliia Klievtsova,Matthias Ehrendorfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向智能体业务流程管理的分类框架，涵盖任务特异性、可追溯性、自主性等关键属性，并提供了定性决策标准和定量评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的智能体在业务流程管理中的应用日益广泛，如何在保持智能体自主性的同时确保系统的鲁棒性、可追溯性和可控性成为关键挑战。当前缺乏系统化的框架来指导和评估智能体编排方案的设计与实现。

Method: 提出了一个分类框架，从任务特异性、可追溯性与可控性、自主性与反应性、正确性保证等维度对智能体编排选项进行分类。提供了定性决策标准用于不同场景实现方案的选择，并设计了定量评估指标。通过预测性光感测场景的不同智能体实现案例进行了验证和演示。

Result: 成功构建了智能体编排的分类框架，明确了各维度的关键属性和决策标准。提出了可行的定量评估指标体系，并通过实际案例展示了框架在预测性光感测场景中的应用效果，验证了所提属性、标准和指标的实用性。

Conclusion: 本工作为智能体编排和代理编排的设计与实现提供了系统化的属性框架、决策标准和评估指标，有助于研究者和实践者在AI智能体自主性与流程控制之间找到合适的平衡点，推动智能体业务流程管理的规范化发展。

Abstract: Agentic Business Process Management has gained momentum recently. The prospect is that the autonomy of AI agents, i.e., predominantly LLM-based agents, can be balanced with a certain level of robustness, tractability, and traceability through a combination with process technology. In this paper, we provide a classification framework for agentic orchestration options along properties such as task specificity, traceability and tractability, autonomy and reactivity, and correctness assurance and present qualitative decision criteria for realizations of different scenarios. We also provide metrics for the quantitative assessment of realization properties and show them through different agentic implementations of a predictive light sensing scenario. Altogether, this work aims at providing properties, criteria, and metrics for the design and implementation of agentic orchestrations and orchestration of agents.

</details>


### [57] [A time-series classification framework for individual-level absenteeism prediction under severe class imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31532)
*Kwong Ho Li,Matthew Roughan,Wathsala Karunarathne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个时间序列分类（TSC）框架用于员工缺勤预测，解决了现有方法无法预测未来事件的局限性。通过实验发现LSTM-FCN混合架构表现最佳，在保留测试数据上实现了约80%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高需求工作环境（如医疗、应急服务、建筑等）中，员工缺勤会带来巨大的运营成本。现有的回归和分类方法存在结构性限制：它们将时间t观察到的特征映射到同一时间t的标签，复制已实现的结果而非预测未来事件，并且丢弃了个人出勤历史中固有的序列行为结构。

Method: 提出时间序列分类（TSC）框架，将历史出勤序列与未来缺勤标签分离，实现真正的前瞻性预测。构建了一个基于UCI数据集校准的可重现模拟数据集。分析了在严重类别不平衡条件下的二元焦点损失（BFL）和几何平均（G-Mean）损失。评估了三种深度学习架构：LSTM、CNN和混合LSTM-FCN。

Result: 对于BFL，平衡权重α≈0.023，达到特异性0.813和平衡准确率0.888，与G-Mean相当。G-Mean无需参数校准即可自动适应。LSTM-FCN在精确度和特异性方面表现强劲。批次大小≥64、窗口大小40-80天时获得稳定性能，在保留测试数据上平衡准确率约80%。

Conclusion: 该TSC框架能够有效预测员工缺勤，LSTM-FCN架构结合适当的批次大小和窗口大小可获得稳定且良好的预测性能，为主动劳动力规划提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Staff absenteeism imposes substantial operational costs in high-demand work environments such as healthcare, emergency services, meat processing, construction, and courier and delivery services, where proactive workforce planning depends on reliable individual-level absence prediction. Existing regression and classification approaches share a structural limitation; they map features observed at time t to labels at the same time t, reproducing already-realised outcomes rather than predicting future events, and discard the sequential behavioural structure inherent in individual attendance histories. We propose a Time Series Classification (TSC) framework that separates historical attendance sequences from future absence labels, enabling genuinely proactive prediction. Due to the lack of public longitudinal attendance data, we construct a reproducible simulated dataset calibrated to the UCI dataset. We analyse Binary Focal Loss (BFL) and Geometric Mean (G-Mean) loss under severe class imbalance using only the imbalance ratio $ρ$. For BFL, the initial gradient ratio is $ρα/(1-α)$, implying the balanced weight $α= 1/(1+ρ) \approx 0.023$. Experiments show that performance is governed mainly by $α$, with BFL achieving specificity 0.813 and balanced accuracy 0.888, comparable to G-Mean. Unlike BFL, G-Mean adapts automatically without parameter calibration. Among three deep learning architectures evaluated, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), and the hybrid LSTM-Fully Convolutional Network (LSTM-FCN), the LSTM-FCN delivers strong precision and specificity. Stable performance is obtained with batch sizes >= 64 and window sizes between 40-80 days, yielding balanced accuracy of approximately 80% on held-out test data.

</details>


### [58] [Modality-Driven Search with Holistic Trace Judging for ARC-AGI-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31543)
*Johan Land*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种ARC-AGI-2视觉推理任务求解器，通过多模态（文本、图像、代码）独立生成候选推理轨迹，并结合上下文保持的整体评判机制，在半私有评估集上达到72.9%的准确率，超越GPT-5.2 Pro（54.2%）和Gemini 3 Pro（54.0%）约18.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在抽象推理任务中能够生成流畅且内部连贯的推理轨迹，但常常出现自信但错误的情况，使得候选选择（而非生成）成为核心挑战。现有的自一致性或多数投票方法无法可靠地恢复正确的少数假设。

Method: 该方法基于两个核心原则：（i）将推理模态视为搜索算子，在文本、图像和代码三个通道上独立生成多样化的候选推理轨迹；（ii）上下文保持的整体评判，即评判模型在单个长上下文提示中联合比较所有候选推理轨迹，而非传统的独立评分或多数投票。

Result: 在ARC Prize半私有评估集上达到72.9%准确率（成本38.99美元/任务），在公开评估集上达到76.1%准确率（成本19.69美元/任务），分别超越最佳独立前沿模型GPT-5.2 Pro（54.2%）和Gemini 3 Pro（54.0%）约18.7个百分点。研究还发现规定性提示模板和迭代细化会系统性降低假设多样性并损害性能。

Conclusion: 多模态独立候选生成与上下文保持的整体评判方法能够可靠地恢复正确的少数假设，在视觉推理任务上显著优于现有的独立前沿模型。该工作开源了完整源代码并记录了大量负面实验结果，为后续研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent, internally coherent reasoning traces for abstract reasoning tasks while still being confidently wrong - making selection among candidates, not just generation, the central challenge. I present a solver for ARC-AGI-2, a few-shot visual reasoning benchmark, built around two principles: (i) treating reasoning modalities as search operators, generating diverse candidates independently across text, image, and code channels, and (ii) context-preserving holistic judging, in which a judge model jointly compares all candidate reasoning traces within a single long-context prompt. Unlike self-consistency or majority voting, this approach reliably recovers correct minority hypotheses on tasks where the modal answer is wrong.
  On the ARC Prize semi-private evaluation set, the solver achieves 72.9 percent at USD 38.99 per task - the highest score on the verified leaderboard at the time of writing, exceeding the best standalone frontier models, GPT-5.2 Pro at 54.2 percent and Gemini 3 Pro at 54.0 percent, by +18.7 percentage points. On the public evaluation set, it achieves 76.1 percent at USD 19.69 per task. I release the full source code and document extensive negative results, including the finding that prescriptive prompting templates and iterative refinement systematically reduce hypothesis diversity and degrade performance.

</details>


### [59] [ACE: Pluggable Adaptive Context Elasticizer across Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31564)
*Ning Liao,Zihao Long,Xiaoxing Wang,Xue Yang,Yaoming Wang,Ziyuan Zhuang,Xunliang Cai,Rongxiang Weng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ACE模块，一种即插即用的自适应上下文弹性管理方法，通过可逆地组织历史信息（原始、摘要或丢弃），解决了LLM智能体在处理长轨迹任务时上下文窗口受限的问题，并在多个框架上取得了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体任务复杂度的增加，轨迹长度快速增长，这对具有固定上下文窗口的大语言模型智能体构成了重大挑战。现有的上下文管理技术（如截断和摘要）存在固有的不可逆性和不灵活性：一旦信息被丢弃或压缩，即使后来变得至关重要也无法恢复。

Method: 提出了自适应上下文弹性器（ACE），这是一个即插即用的模块，在每个决策步骤弹性地组织历史信息。ACE包含两层：1) 无损消息维护层，存储每个历史步骤的原始消息和压缩摘要；2) 上下文编排层，根据当前任务状态自适应地为每个步骤分配弹性类型（原始、摘要或丢弃）。这种可逆设计确保主LLM始终接收紧凑但信息丰富的上下文。该方法无需训练或架构修改即可适配现有框架。

Result: 将ACE适配到四个不同的智能体框架（ReAct、DeepAgent、WebThinker和MiroFlow）上进行实验。结果显示，ACE始终优于截断和摘要基线方法，并在所有四个智能体框架上带来了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: ACE成功解决了现有上下文管理技术的不可逆性问题，通过可逆的弹性上下文管理机制，在保持上下文紧凑的同时确保关键信息的可恢复性，为LLM智能体处理长轨迹任务提供了一种有效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of agentic tasks has led to rapidly growing trajectory lengths, which poses significant challenges for large language model (LLM) based agents with fixed context windows. Existing context management techniques, such as truncation and summarization, suffer from inherent inflexibility and irreversibility: once information is discarded or compressed, it cannot be recovered even when it becomes critically relevant in later decision steps. To address these limitations, we propose the Adaptive Context Elasticizer (ACE), a plug-and-play module that elastically orchestrates historical step information into the agent's context at each decision step. ACE maintains a lossless message maintenance layer that stores both raw messages and compressed abstractions for each historical step, while a context orchestration layer adaptively assigns each step an elastic type as raw, abstract, or drop, at every decision step based on the current task state. This reversible design ensures that the main LLM always receives a compact yet information-rich context. We adapt ACE to four diverse agent frameworks, including ReAct, DeepAgent, WebThinker, and MiroFlow, without training or architectural modifications. Experiments show that ACE consistently outperforms truncation and summarization baselines, and brings consistent performance gains across all four agent frameworks.

</details>


### [60] [Which Tokens Matter? Adaptive Token Selection for RLVR with the Relative Surprisal Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31575)
*Outongyi Lv,Yanzhao Zheng,Yuanwei Zhang,Zhenghao Huang,Xingjun Wang,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Yingda Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了相对惊奇指数(RSI)，这是一种基于信息论的新指标，通过耦合token的熵和概率来解决强化学习中token选择的矛盾观点，并基于此开发了RSI-S方法，在数学推理任务上比GRPO提升了2-3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)在LLM推理方面取得了成功，但近期研究提出了矛盾的观点：一方主张优先训练高熵token位置，另一方则警告不应让低概率token主导梯度更新。由于高熵token通常与低概率相关，这两种看似矛盾的范式都能带来性能提升。本文认为单独评估采样token的概率或熵不足以捕捉策略优化动态，需要一种新的指标来解决这一张力。

Method: 提出了相对惊奇指数(RSI)，这是一种基于信息论的指标，自然地耦合了token的熵与所选token的概率。在温和条件下，RSI与所选logit扰动下logit梯度范数一阶变差与预测熵的局部比值相关。基于RSI，提出了RSI Selection(RSI-S)方法，这是一种熵自适应token过滤方法，保留稳定RSI区间内的token，过滤掉冗余的低惊奇token和不稳定的高惊奇尾部token。

Result: RSI-S成功调和了先前矛盾的范式。在不同模型规模(Qwen2.5-1.5B、3B和7B)的AIME和AMC基准测试中，RSI-S相比GRPO在avg@32准确率上提升了2-3个百分点。实验表明该方法在不同模型规模上都能取得更高的avg@32准确率。

Conclusion: RSI为RLVR改进提供了一个有前景的视角。该工作通过引入信息论指标成功解决了现有研究中关于token选择的矛盾观点，证明了结合token熵和概率的重要性，为LLM的强化学习训练提供了新的理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful tool for propelling Large Language Models (LLMs) beyond imitation-based training towards more robust reasoning capabilities. Among existing approaches, RL with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm for advancing LLM reasoning. Despite its empirical success, recent studies have offered different insights. One line of inquiry advocates prioritizing high-entropy token positions during training, while another perspective cautions against allowing low-probability tokens to dominate gradient updates. Notably, although high-entropy tokens are usually correlated with low probability, both paradigms empirically yield substantial performance gains. In this work, we argue that evaluating sampled-token probability or entropy in isolation is insufficient to capture the policy optimization dynamics. To resolve this tension, we introduce the Relative Surprisal Index (RSI), a principled, information-theoretic metric that naturally couples the token's entropy with the probability of the selected token. We show that, under mild conditions, RSI is related to the local ratio between the first-order variations of the logit-gradient norm and predictive entropy under a selected-logit perturbation. Building on RSI, we propose RSI Selection (RSI-S), an entropy-adaptive token filtering method that retains tokens within a stable RSI interval. RSI-S successfully reconciles previous contradictory paradigms and filters out both redundant low-surprisal tokens and unstable high-surprisal tail tokens. Empirical evaluations show that RSI-S achieves higher avg@32 accuracy across different model scales (Qwen2.5-1.5B, 3B, and 7B) on AIME and AMC benchmarks: RSI-S improves avg@32 accuracy by 2--3 percentage points over GRPO. Overall, RSI offers a promising perspective for RLVR improvement.

</details>


### [61] [Scientific Explanations in Health Sciences: Causality, Trust, and Epistemic Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31616)
*Martina Mattioli,Marcello Pelillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从科学哲学视角审视医学可解释AI（XAI），通过整合因果关系、信任维度和解释充分性标准，提出设计符合临床需求的XAI系统原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学AI决策过程的不透明性促使可解释性研究兴起，但关于何为充分的医学解释尚无定论。现有XAI研究忽视了科学哲学和医学哲学中关于解释的长期理论积累，导致其基础假设缺乏检验，亟需跨学科视角填补这一空白。

Method: 采用跨学科批判性综述方法，系统考察健康科学中的解释理论，评估其对医学XAI的适用性；围绕三个核心分析维度展开：因果关系在医学推理中的作用、医学信任的认知与关系维度、以及由多元利益相关者实际需求塑造的解释充分性标准。

Result: 明确了医学XAI解释的必要哲学条件，确立了因果性、信任机制和解释充分性作为评估医学解释的核心维度；提出了面向临床决策的XAI设计原则，这些原则既保证认知稳健性，又与临床实践的认知和实用要求相契合。

Conclusion: 医学可解释AI研究需要以哲学为基础重新构建其概念框架，解释的充分性标准必须同时满足认知合理性和临床实际需求；哲学洞察为设计真正服务于临床决策的XAI系统提供了理论根基，推动了医学XAI研究向尚未充分探索的概念基础方向发展。

Abstract: Medical Artificial Intelligence (AI) is widely expected to transform clinical practice, yet the decision-making processes of many Machine Learning (ML) models remain opaque. Explainability has been advanced as a partial remedy to clarify why AI generates predictions, particularly in high-stakes contexts. Despite ongoing efforts, debates on what constitutes an adequate medical explanation remain unsettled. Yet, explanation has long been a central topic of inquiry in the philosophy of science and medicine. The insights developed in these fields, however, have been largely overlooked in contemporary explainable AI (XAI) research, leaving its foundational assumptions insufficiently examined. To address this gap, this paper develops a critical review at the intersection of philosophy of science and XAI. It examines prevailing accounts of what counts as an explanation in the health sciences and assesses their adequacy for informing XAI in medicine, arguing that they provide necessary conditions for a philosophically grounded approach to explainability in this domain. Building on this foundational philosophical literature, the discussion identifies three central axes of analysis: the role of causality in medical reasoning, the epistemic and relational dimensions of medical trust, and the criteria of explanatory adequacy as shaped by the pragmatic needs of diverse stakeholders. By integrating philosophical analysis with current developments in medical AI, the paper outlines principles for designing XAI systems that offer explanations that are not only epistemically robust but also aligned with the epistemic and practical requirements of clinical decision-making, shaping ongoing debates in medical XAI toward underexplored conceptual foundations.

</details>


### [62] [Think in English, Answer in Korean: Efficient Adaptation of Multilingual Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31648)
*Utsav Garg,Sungjin Hong,Jason Jung,Justin Lee,Shaan Desai,Joon Hee Kim,Anirudh Shrinivason,Edmond Wen,Susie Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LuckyStar 111B，一个由Cohere和LG CNS合作开发的111B参数混合推理模型，专为韩英企业智能体设计，在受限内存和服务条件下实现了高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际内存和服务约束下，开发能够高效执行可验证智能体工作流程的韩英双语企业智能体模型，解决多语言模型在工具使用和多步推理任务中的适应性问题。

Method: 基于Cohere完全后训练的Command A模型进行训练，采用前导条件控制简洁非推理行为与长程工具导向推理之间的切换。研究四种高效扩展方法：多语言监督微调、可验证奖励的强化学习用于多步工具使用任务、韩语用户响应的语言一致性奖励，以及支持单GPU服务的4-bit量化。

Result: 改进后的模型在数学推理、函数调用和智能体自然语言转SQL（NL2SQL）性能方面取得提升，同时保持了韩语和英语通用指令遵循质量。

Conclusion: 为在内存受限部署环境下适应后训练多语言模型到可验证智能体工作流程提供了实用的解决方案和失败模式分析。

Abstract: We present LuckyStar 111B, a 111B-parameter hybrid reasoning model developed through a collaboration between Cohere and LG CNS for Korean-English enterprise agents under practical memory and serving constraints. The model trains from Cohere's fully post-trained Command A model rather than a new pretraining run, and uses preamble conditioning to switch between concise non-reasoning behavior and longer tool-oriented reasoning. We study four choices for scaling tool-using agents efficiently: multilingual supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for multi-step tool-use tasks, language-consistency rewards for Korean user-facing responses, and 4-bit quantization for single-GPU serving. The adapted model improves mathematical reasoning, function calling, and agentic natural-language-to-SQL (NL2SQL) performance while preserving general Korean and English instruction-following quality. These results provide a practical recipe and failure-mode analysis for adapting post-trained multilingual models to verifiable agentic workflows under memory-constrained deployment.

</details>


### [63] [FARS: A Fully Automated Research System Deployed at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31651)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FARS，一个面向AI/ML领域的全自动研究系统，能够在无需人工干预的情况下，大规模自主完成从选题、规划、实验到论文写作的全流程研究工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化研究系统大多依赖人工设定的主题、少量预定义任务或精心挑选的成功案例来展示效果，缺乏大规模、多样化主题下的全自动研究验证。作者希望构建一个能在大规模真实场景中运行的完全自动化AI研究系统，以检验其可行性和局限性。

Method: FARS采用阶段特定的智能体协作架构，包括构思、规划、实验和写作四个阶段。系统通过共享工作空间协调各阶段智能体，记录并管理研究提案、代码、日志、实验结果和论文手稿等中间产物，形成可审计的研究语料库。

Result: 首次公开部署中，FARS自主生成了166篇完整的AI/ML研究论文，涵盖67个细粒度研究主题。282份志愿者评审（覆盖140篇论文）显示，FARS能够产出具备评审价值、偶尔达到较高水平的AI/ML研究成果；但同时也暴露出实验范围狭窄、方法论局限、完整性问题等系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了在真实大规模部署场景下，完全自动化的AI研究系统确实可行，能够产出有价值的科研产物。然而，要实现稳定、高质量的研究输出，仍需解决实验设计深度、方法论严谨性和研究完整性等关键挑战。系统产出的完整语料库而非精选案例，为后续研究提供了真实评估基准。

Abstract: Recent automated research systems show that language-model agents can generate hypotheses, run experiments, and write complete manuscripts, but most evidence still comes from selected examples, human-framed topics, or a few pre-defined research tasks. We present FARS (Fully Automated Research System), a fully automated AI-for-AI research system designed to operate across research topics at scale. FARS autonomously generates and advances projects through ideation, planning, experimentation, and writing, using stage-specific agents coordinated through a shared workspace that records proposals, code, logs, results, and manuscripts. In its first public deployment, FARS produced 166 complete research papers spanning 67 fine-grained AI/ML topics while preserving intermediate artifacts as an auditable corpus rather than a curated set of successes. We evaluate this corpus with 282 structured reviews from volunteer reviewers covering 140 papers, including overall ratings, sub-scores, integrity checks, and LLM-use disclosure. The reviews indicate that FARS can produce review-worthy and occasionally strong AI/ML research artifacts in a large-scale public deployment, while also exposing recurring failure modes in narrow experimental scope, methodological limitations, and integrity issues.

</details>


### [64] [Arena-T2I Hard: Benchmarking and Improving Faithfulness with Dependency-Aware Checklist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31711)
*Yuanhao Ban,Tong Xie,Sohyun An,Yunqi Hong,Evan Frick,I-Hung Hsu,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了Arena-T2I Hard基准测试和依赖感知检查表奖励方法，解决了现有T2I模型在复杂提示词忠实度评估和优化方面的不足，在SD3.5-Medium和FLUX.1-dev上实现了更好的忠实度-美观性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I忠实度基准测试依赖简单原子指令，顶尖系统已接近满分，缺乏区分度。实际创意工作流中用户提出复杂多面请求（空间关系、风格约束、文本渲染），单一二元VLM评判分数无法捕捉模型违反的具体约束。此外，公共竞技场排名优先考虑美观性而非细粒度提示词遵循，高排名无法预测忠实度。

Method: 1) 构建Arena-T2I Hard：310个来自真实竞技场日志的压力测试提示词，每个提示词分解为约30个是/否约束（涵盖6个类别包括文本渲染）；2) 提出依赖感知检查表奖励：将提示词分解为有向无环图(DAG)形式的约束，失败父节点的后代约束置零，生成每约束训练信号；3) 通过组解耦归一化(GDPO)将检查表奖励与BT美观奖励结合，在各rollout组内标准化奖励以避免任一奖励坍塌。

Result: Arena-T2I Hard在11个系统间产生33个百分点的性能差距，最强闭源系统仅达0.855，展现出显著区分力。高公共竞技场排名无法预测忠实度。提出的方法在SD3.5-Medium和FLUX.1-dev上，经MMRB2成对比较验证，在忠实度-美观性权衡方面严格优于所有单一奖励、朴素加权和及4奖励BT集成基线。

Conclusion: Arena-T2I Hard为复杂提示词场景提供了有效的忠实度评估基准，依赖感知检查表奖励结合GDPO的方法成功实现了忠实度与美观性的平衡优化，为T2I模型在创意工作流中的应用提供了更可靠的评估和训练框架。

Abstract: Faithfulness -- how precisely a generated image aligns with its prompt -- is increasingly central to the real-world utility of text-to-image (T2I) models. Existing faithfulness benchmarks, however, rely on simple atomic instructions, on which top-tier systems already achieve near-perfect scores. As T2I models enter creative workflows, users issue multi-faceted requests combining intricate spatial relationships, stylistic constraints, and complex text rendering. In this setting, a single binary VLM-judge score no longer captures which specific constraints the model fails to satisfy. We introduce Arena-T2I Hard, a 310-prompt stress benchmark drawn from real arena T2I logs, with approximately 30 decomposed yes/no constraints per prompt spanning six categories, including text rendering. The strongest closed-source system we evaluate reaches 0.855 with a 33~pp performance gap across 11 systems, demonstrating substantial discriminative power. Moreover, high public-arena rankings fail to predict faithfulness, confirming that holistic Bradley-Terry (BT) preference scores prioritize aesthetics over fine-grained prompt adherence. We propose a dependency-aware checklist reward that decomposes each prompt into a DAG of yes/no questions and zeroes descendants of failed parents, turning faithfulness into a per-constraint training signal. Combined with a BT aesthetic reward via group-decoupled normalization (GDPO), which standardizes each reward within its rollout group so neither collapses, the recipe attains a strictly better faithfulness-aesthetics trade-off on SD3.5-Medium and FLUX.1-dev under MMRB2 pairwise comparisons than every single-reward, naive weighted-sum, or 4-reward BT-ensemble baseline.

</details>


### [65] [A Self-Evolving Agentic System for Automated Generation and Execution of Biological Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31763)
*Yankai Jiang,Weiting Tang,Haoran Sun,Zhenyu Tang,Yuejie Hou,Yingnan Han,Rubo Wang,Yueyuxiao Yang,Cheng Liang,Lilong Wang,Wenjie Lou,Xiaosong Wang,Lei Bai,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProtoPilot，一个自进化的多智能体系统，用于将生物实验协议转换为可执行的代码，在294个合成生物学和分子生物学任务上达到90.2%的专家偏好率和88.24%的实验执行成功率，并经过真实湿实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主湿实验不仅需要合理的协议文本，还需要在从协议和SOP设计到代码和物理执行的整个过程中，保持生物意图、定量程序、设备约束和实验反馈的一致性。现有的方法无法有效解决这一复杂的转换问题。

Method: 开发了ProtoPilot自进化多智能体系统，包含分层可验证性、多智能体编排和运行时更新的技能库；建立了基于专家的基准测试框架，涵盖294个任务、湿实验专家评分标准、设备级有效性检查和真实实验测试；系统能够生成协议、扩展SOP、合成SDK兼容代码，并根据湿实验反馈修正工作流程。

Result: ProtoPilot实现了90.2%的Top@3专家偏好率，89.5%的协议到代码门控通过率，以及88.24%的Opentrons通过率（对比OpenTrons-AI的32.35%）；湿实验验证产生了可解释的读数、Sanger确认的产物和反馈修正的PCA组装DNA目标。

Conclusion: 评估框架成功捕获了自主湿实验自动化所需的执行相关要求，ProtoPilot通过将协议和代码生成转换为验证执行和反馈引导的修订，能够满足这些要求，建立了通往自主实验的可验证路径。

Abstract: Autonomous wet-lab experimentation requires more than plausible protocol text: biological intent, quantitative procedures, device constraints and experimental feedback must remain aligned from protocol and SOP design to code and physical execution. We developed ProtoPilot, a self-evolving multi-agent system, together with an expert-grounded benchmark and evaluation framework for testing this conversion as an experimental automation problem. The framework spans 294 synthetic-biology and molecular-biology tasks derived from 98 gold-standard protocols, wet-lab expert rubrics, device-level validity gates and real experimental tests. ProtoPilot incorporates layer-wise verifiability, multi-agent orchestration and a runtime-updated skill library to generate protocols, expand SOPs, synthesize SDK-compliant code and revise workflows from wet-lab feedback. It achieved a Top@3 expert-preference rate of 90.2%, an overall protocol-to-code gate pass rate of 89.5% and an Opentrons pass rate of 88.24%, compared with 32.35% for OpenTrons-AI. Wet-lab validation produced interpretable readouts, Sanger-confirmed products and feedback-corrected PCA-assembled DNA targets, establishing a verifiable route to autonomous experimentation. Together, these results show that the evaluation framework captures execution-relevant requirements for autonomous wet-lab automation, and that ProtoPilot can meet them by converting protocol and code generation into validated execution and feedback-guided revision.

</details>


### [66] [Evo-PI: Aligning Medical Reasoning via Evolving Principle-Guided Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31800)
*Xianda Zheng,Huan Gao,Meng-Fen Chiang,Michael Witbrock,Kaiqi Zhao,Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Evo-PI框架，通过将推理原则作为可动态演化的监督信号，实现原则与模型行为的协同进化，在医学视觉问答任务上相比静态监督方法提升高达24.6%的推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态语言模型（MLLMs）在推理能力上受限于静态监督方法，固定的提示、规则或奖励模型无法自适应地指导训练过程，导致在复杂决策任务中泛化能力脆弱且性能饱和。静态信号虽能约束输出格式，但无法有效塑造底层推理过程。

Method: 提出Evo-PI框架，采用以原则为中心的学习方法，将推理原则作为显式的、基于语言的监督信号。构建协同进化循环：原则指导模型推理，模型行为反过来优化监督原则。这种动态对齐机制使监督能够逐步适应模型的推理缺陷。在医学视觉问答领域进行实例验证。

Result: 在8个基准测试和多个模型骨干网络上进行实验，Evo-PI在推理准确率上取得了一致的提升，最高增益达到24.6%。证明了演化原则引导的监督方法在MLLMs专家级推理训练中的有效性。

Conclusion: 演化式原则引导监督为训练MLLMs的专家对齐推理提供了可扩展且通用的范式，突破了静态监督的性能瓶颈，为复杂推理任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, the reasoning capabilities of large multimodal language models (MLLMs) remain fundamentally constrained by static supervision, where fixed prompts, rules, or reward models provide non-adaptive guidance throughout training. Such static signals are often sufficient to enforce output formats, but fail to shape the underlying reasoning process, leading to brittle generalization and performance saturation in complex decision-making tasks. We propose Evo-PI, a principle-centric learning framework that treats reasoning principles as explicit, language-based supervision signals that can be generated, evaluated, and iteratively evolved. Instead of relying on fixed rewards, Evo-PI enables a co-evolutionary loop in which principles guide model reasoning, while model behaviors in turn refine the principles that supervise them. This dynamic alignment mechanism allows supervision to progressively adapt to the model's reasoning deficiencies. We instantiate Evo-PI in medical visual question answering as a high-stakes testbed requiring structured visual-textual reasoning. Across eight benchmarks and multiple model backbones, Evo-PI consistently improves reasoning accuracy, achieving gains of up to 24.6%. Our results suggest that evolving principle-guided supervision offers a scalable and general paradigm for training expert-aligned reasoning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/zhengxianda/Evo_PI.

</details>


### [67] [RAISE: LLM-based Automated Heuristic Design with Robust Adversary Instance Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31801)
*Fei Liu,Alessio Figalli,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAISE框架，通过在LLM驱动的启发式自动设计（AHD）中引入受限对抗实例搜索，解决现有方法在分布偏移下的性能崩溃问题，在多个组合优化问题上显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的启发式自动设计（AHD）方法针对固定的训练实例集优化启发式算法，在部署时面临现实世界的分布偏移会导致性能急剧下降（高达19倍），缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出鲁棒对抗实例搜索（RAISE）框架，将鲁棒AHD建模为受限对抗实例搜索问题：外循环通过LLM算子进化启发式算法，内循环（无LLM）使用基分布参数化与边界投影方法，在训练实例集的ε-邻域内高效识别困难实例。

Result: 在在线装箱问题（OBP）、在线作业车间调度（OJSP）和在线车辆路径问题（OVRP）上，跨越五种分布族的全面实验表明，现有LLM-AHD方法在分布偏移下性能下降高达19倍，而RAISE在所有测试分布和问题规模上均保持强性能。

Conclusion: RAISE通过将受限最坏情况实例搜索集成到LLM进化搜索循环中，有效解决了LLM-based AHD方法在分布偏移下的脆弱性问题，实现了跨分布和规模的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Automated Heuristic Design (AHD) with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown remarkable progress in discovering high-quality heuristics. However, existing LLM-based AHD methods optimize heuristics for a fixed training instance set and may fail catastrophically when deployed under real-world distributional shifts. We propose Robust Adversary Instance Search (RAISE), a framework that integrates constrained worst-case instance search within a principled neighborhood of the training distribution into the LLM-based evolutionary search loop. RAISE treats robust AHD as a constrained adversarial instance search problem: the outer loop evolves heuristics via LLM operators, while an LLM-free inner loop efficiently identifies hard instances within an epsilon-ball around the training instance set using a basis distribution parameterization with boundary projection. Comprehensive experiments on Online Bin Packing (OBP), Online Job Shop Scheduling (OJSP), and Online Vehicle Routing (OVRP) across five distribution families demonstrate that existing LLM-based AHD methods degrade by up to 19 times under distribution shift, while RAISE consistently maintains strong performance across all tested distributions and problem scales

</details>


### [68] [Large Databases Need Small, Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31808)
*Parker Glenn,Alfy Samuel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了在16GB显存本地运行的量化开源模型，能够在更低延迟和成本下达到或超越闭源API模型的准确率，实现成本降低390倍、延迟降低3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于专有API的语言模型系统采用基于token的计费模式，在大数据库环境下成本极高，单次实验成本可能超过1万美元，严重阻碍了LM-数据库集成的研究与实际部署。

Method: 研究量化开源模型在16GB显存本地环境的高效部署，开发关键系统优化技术，并将这些本地模型集成到BlendSQL v0.1.0框架中。

Result: 本地量化开源模型在准确率上匹配或超越闭源API模型，同时实现成本降低390倍、延迟降低3.8倍。

Conclusion: 闭源API模型并非LM-数据库集成的必要条件，经过优化的本地开源模型是更具性价比的可行替代方案，挑战了业界对闭源API的依赖假设。

Abstract: Language model systems built around proprietary APIs often operate on a token-based cost model. This becomes prohibitively expensive in the context of large databases, where LM-enhanced relational operators can incur costs exceeding $10,000 for a single set of experiments, hindering thorough research and practical deployment. In this paper, we demonstrate that quantized, open-weight models running locally on just 16GB of VRAM can match or exceed the accuracy of closed-source counterparts at lower latency and a fraction of the price, challenging the prevailing assumption that closed-source LM APIs are necessary for effective LM-database integration. We present and analyze the key system optimizations required to efficiently deploy these open-weight models within an LM-DB system. By integrating these local models into the BlendSQL v0.1.0 framework, we demonstrate a 390x reduction in overall costs and 3.8x reduction in latency compared to a proprietary LM API. We make our code available at https://github.com/CapitalOne-Research/play-by-the-type-rules/tree/main/sembench.

</details>


### [69] [Creating Intelligence: A Computational Foundation for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31819)
*Peter Overmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于集合论和超维计算的离散二值认知计算理论，通过拓扑可塑性实现联想记忆，统一自联想与异联想学习，并映射到小脑和新皮层实现高效硬件化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络依赖连续权重和矩阵乘法，存在连续性瓶颈且难以直接建模生物神经种群编码；需要一种与生物神经离散稀疏特性更契合的计算框架。

Method: 以稀疏二值集合为基本表征，构建具有组合扩展隐藏层的网络拓扑；学习由拓扑可塑性驱动，核心算法为子集模式匹配与精确最近邻搜索，实现自联想与异联想学习的统一。

Result: 在网络拓扑中自然涌现联想记忆能力，实现常数时间复杂度的信息检索，成功桥接感知稀疏分布式表征与符号稀疏全息表征，可直接映射到内存硬件。

Conclusion: 小脑与新皮层可能均实现子集模式匹配变体，使其成为认知的基本引擎；离散逻辑替代矩阵运算为合成智能提供了人脑级能效的新路径。

Abstract: This work introduces a new computational theory of mind grounded in set theory and hyperdimensional computing. Whereas traditional neural networks rely on continuous weights and matrix multiplication, this framework works with sparse binary data. It represents information as discrete sets, directly modeling biological neural population codes. I demonstrate that associative memory emerges naturally from network topologies featuring a combinatorially expanded hidden layer. Learning is driven by topological plasticity rather than scalar weight adjustments. This architecture unifies auto-associative and hetero-associative learning under a single core algorithm: information retrieval via subset pattern matching and exact nearest-neighbor search. Operating with constant-time complexity, these mechanisms bridge perceptual data (sparse distributed representations) and symbols (sparse holographic representations) without continuous bottlenecks. Mapping this framework to neuroanatomy, I propose that both the cerebellum and the neocortex implement variants of this algorithm, making subset pattern matching the fundamental engine of cognition. Because it relies on discrete logic rather than matrix arithmetic, this algorithm translates directly into in-memory hardware. This opens a new route toward synthetic intelligence with human-level energy efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [Adaptive Cluster-First Route-Second Decomposition for Industrial-Scale Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31820)
*Oguzhan Karaahmetoglu,Hyong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应的“先聚类后路径规划”（CFRS）系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）作为高层决策者动态分解大规模带容量车辆路径问题（CVRP），在高达50万客户的实例上展现出良好的可扩展性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CVRP分解方法通常依赖固定的划分规则、预定义的优化目标或学习的策略，在面对具有不同空间、需求和操作特征的实例时表现不稳定，难以适应多样化的实际场景。

Method: 提出一种自适应CFRS系统，将分解过程建模为迭代决策过程；引入LLM作为高层决策器，分析当前分解状态并自适应选择聚类、平衡和精细化算子；联合划分客户与车辆，实现容量感知聚类，根据问题特征动态调整划分决策。

Result: 在合成数据和基准CVRP实例（规模达500,000客户）上验证了方法的有效性；在基准规模实例上具有竞争力的表现，在更大规模问题上展现出更好的可扩展性和路径质量鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应、LLM引导的决策支持方法为工业级车辆路径规划和大规模物流规划提供了一种切实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale capacitated vehicle routing problems (CVRPs) are commonly addressed using cluster-first route-second (CFRS) approaches that split a routing instance into smaller, computationally tractable subproblems. Existing splitting methods typically rely on fixed partitioning rules, predefined optimization objectives, or learned policies, which may perform inconsistently across instances exhibiting different spatial, demand, and operational characteristics. In this work, we propose an adaptive CFRS system that formulates a decomposition procedure as an iterative decision-making process. Motivated by the recent success of large language models (LLMs) in reasoning and tool selection, the system employs an LLM as a high-level decision maker that analyzes the evolving decomposition state and selectively applies further clustering, balancing, and refinement operators. The proposed algorithm jointly partitions customers and vehicles, enabling capacity-aware clustering while adapting partitioning decisions to the characteristics of each problem. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and benchmark-derived CVRP instances containing up to 500,000 customers. Experimental results demonstrate competitive performance on benchmark-scale instances while exhibiting improved scalability and robust routing quality on substantially larger problems. These results highlight the potential of adaptive, LLM-guided decision support as a practical approach for industrial-scale vehicle routing and large-scale logistics planning.

</details>


### [71] [An Agentic AI Framework to Accelerate Scientific Discovery in Plant Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31831)
*Renan Souza,Daniel Rosendo,Kelsey Carter,John Lagergren,Frédéric Suter,Shelaine L. Curd,Gerald A. Tuskan,Rafael Ferreira da Silva,David Weston*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的智能体AI框架，将高通量植物表型分析设施从数据工厂转变为交互式自主发现平台，将原本数天到数周的分析过程缩短至秒级交互循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量植物表型分析设施每天生成大量图像数据，但性状提取和解释仍依赖人工专家操作，分析过程成为制约科学发现的瓶颈。现有的云原生智能体框架忽略了联合计算、数据移动和溯源记录等现实问题。

Method: 开发了一个双智能体框架：(1)对话式Co-Scientist Agent，将科学家的自然语言问题转化为结构化分析计划；(2)无头Compute Agent，在Frontier超算上调度Vision Transformer分割和性状提取任务。两个智能体在独立的安全和资源域中运行，通过安全的令牌认证流式通道进行通信，确保每次交互的端到端溯源记录。

Result: 该框架成功将原本需要数天到数周的分析过程转变为交互式循环，智能体能够在数秒内对结果进行推理、推荐后续分析并响应后续问题，显著加速了科学洞察的获取时间。

Conclusion: 提出的智能体AI框架有效解决了高通量植物表型分析中的分析瓶颈问题，通过人机协作将数据工厂转变为交互式发现平台，为加速植物科学发现提供了可行的技术解决方案。

Abstract: High-throughput plant phenotyping now generates image derived datasets far faster than scientists can analyze them. At Oak Ridge National Laboratory's Advanced Plant Phenotyping Laboratory (APPL), automated stations image hundreds of plants daily across multiple remote sensing modalities; yet, trait extraction and interpretation remain manual, expert-bound, and strictly post-hoc, making analysis, not acquisition, the binding constraint on discovery. We present an end-to-end agentic AI framework that turns the facility from a data factory into an interactive autonomous, discovery platform, where scientists partner with AI agents to accelerate time to insight. A conversational Co-Scientist Agent translates a scientist's natural-language question into a structured analysis plan, and a headless Compute Agent dispatches Vision Transformer segmentation and trait extraction on the Frontier exascale supercomputer. The two agents run in separate security and resource domains and communicate over a secure, token-authenticated streaming channel, a design that accounts for the federation, data-movement, and provenance realities cloud-native agentic frameworks ignore, ensuring end-to-end provenance is captured for every interaction. The framework turns a days- to weeks-long analysis process into an interactive loop where agents reason over results, recommend next analyses, and respond to follow-up questions in seconds.

</details>


### [72] [Harnessing Textual Refusal Directions for Multimodal Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31876)
*Moreno D'Incà,Massimiliano Mancini,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS方法，利用文本模态提取的拒绝方向实现多模态大模型的免训练安全对齐，无需多模态安全数据即可有效提升模型安全性并保持实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的安全对齐方法依赖不安全的多模态数据，这类数据比单模态数据更难收集。本文旨在探索是否可以直接从LLM主干网络中提取文本拒绝方向，并使其跨模态（图像、视频）泛化，从而放松对多模态安全数据的需求约束。

Method: 提出模态无关拒绝引导方法(MARS)：1) 通过激活重新居中校正模态错位，避免安全多模态输入被错误拒绝；2) 在几何定义的信任域内自适应调整引导强度；3) 选择最优干预层；4) 在首个生成token处操作。该方法是轻量级且免训练的。

Result: 在五个SOTA多模态大模型上进行评估，涵盖安全性、实用性和视频越狱基准测试。MARS在保持实用性的同时实现了持续的安全增益，证明了安全相关结构在模态间共享，且文本拒绝方向是多模态对齐的强大基础。

Conclusion: 安全性相关的表征结构在不同模态间共享，文本拒绝方向为多模态对齐提供了强大且未被充分探索的基础。MARS证明了无需多模态安全数据即可实现有效的跨模态安全引导。

Abstract: To improve safety in Large Language Models (LLMs) we can either perform post-training alignment or exploit refusal directions in the activation space. Both strategies are less feasible in Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) as they require unsafe multimodal data, harder to collect than their unimodal counterpart. In this work, we relax this constraint and investigate whether textual refusal directions, extracted directly from the LLM backbone, generalize across modalities (i.e., image, video). Preliminary findings confirm this ability, though effectiveness is conditioned by layer selection, steering strength, and cross-modal alignment, with the latter causing safe multimodal inputs to be spuriously steered toward refusal. Building on this, we introduce Modality-Agnostic Refusal Steering (MARS), a light-weight training-free approach that injects multimodal safety without the need for multimodal safety data. MARS corrects modality misalignment via activation re-centering, adaptively scales steering strength within a geometrically defined trust region, and selects the optimal intervention layer, operating at the first generated token. Evaluated on five SOTA MLLMs across safety, utility, and video jailbreak benchmarks, MARS achieves consistent safety gains while preserving utility. These results reveal that safety-relevant structure is shared across modalities and that textual refusal directions are a powerful and underexplored foundation for multimodal alignment.

</details>


### [73] [Self-Study Reconsidered: The Hidden Fragility of Learning from Self-Generated QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32002)
*Ekaterina Alimaskina,Denis Shveykin,Gleb Molodtsov,Igor Shalygin,Alexey Kadeishvili,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型在合成问答数据生成过程中的脆弱性：生成器在选择提问内容时存在偏见（过早饱和、集中在显著片段），在回答时容易受到文本中指令性内容的影响。作者提出了两种无需改变训练流程的缓解方法：将问题绑定固定目标以减少选择偏见，以及在回答前过滤指令性片段（将注入合规率从88%降至13%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型越来越多地使用合成问答数据进行微调、蒸馏或知识压缩，作者发现这一生成步骤并非中立的预处理过程，而是一个隐式策略，既选择哪些证据成为训练信号，又决定如何回答这些证据。研究动机在于揭示这一过程中的脆弱性并提出缓解方案。

Method: 作者系统分析了合成问答生成的两个阶段：（1）选择提问内容时的行为模式，考察生成器如何扫描文档、覆盖饱和度、收敛模式以及对局部呈现形式的依赖；（2）回答生成的问题时的行为，特别是对文本中嵌入的指令性内容的服从情况。基于发现的失败模式，提出了两种缓解策略：将每个问题绑定固定目标以减少选择偏见，以及在回答前过滤指令性片段。

Result: 实验发现：（1）生成器在选题时不均匀扫描文档，覆盖过早饱和并集中在显著片段，不同提示词收敛到相同区域，局部呈现形式驱动问题的生成，导致显著的人为标记（如清理不当的标记）可劫持跨模型家族和规模的问题生成；（2）在回答时，模型倾向于服从文本中嵌入的指令性片段，这种服从取决于意图和表面形式而非严格程度，在任务冲突时最严重，且更大模型更频繁服从。通过绑定固定目标和过滤指令性片段，可将注入合规率从88%降至13%，同时保留几乎所有清洁文本。

Conclusion: 合成问答生成过程中的失败模式源于生成阶段的选择，因此可以在不改变训练循环的情况下进行缓解。提出的方法有效地减少了选择偏见和指令注入问题，为构建更可靠的合成数据生成流程提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly taught from synthetic question--answer (QA) supervision: a model generates questions about a document, answers them from the same text, and the resulting pairs are used to fine-tune, distill, or compress knowledge into another model. We show that this generation step is not neutral preprocessing. It is an implicit policy that both selects which evidence becomes training signal and decides how that evidence is answered, and it is fragile at both stages. When choosing what to ask, generators do not scan a document uniformly. Coverage saturates early and concentrates on salient spans, diverse prompts converge on the same regions, and what looks question-worthy is driven by local presentation. As a result, salient artifacts such as poorly cleaned markup can hijack question generation across model families and scales. When answering, the model that produces the supervision tends to obey instruction-like passages embedded in the text. This compliance depends on the intent and surface form of the passage rather than its strictness, and is worst under task conflict, where larger models comply more often. These failure modes arise from choices made during QA generation, so they can be reduced without changing the training loop. Tying each question to a fixed target reduces biased selection, and filtering instruction-like spans before answering lowers mean injection compliance from $88\%$ to $13\%$ in our evaluation while retaining nearly all clean text.

</details>


### [74] [PolicyGuard: From Organizational Policies to Neuro-SymbolicCompliance Review Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32004)
*Sameer Malik,Ayush Singh,Amar Prakash Azad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PolicyGuard，一个用于策略导向文档合规审查的神经符号框架，通过将组织策略转换为可执行逻辑规则，结合LLM的局部文档解释和符号评估器，实现了显式、可维护且可系统测试的合规决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端LLM提示方法在处理策略导向文档审查时，将应用的策略逻辑隐式化，导致合规决策难以检查、更新和测试。组织需要能够明确追踪策略应用逻辑、便于维护和系统化测试的文档合规审查方案。

Method: 提出PolicyGuard神经符号框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)将组织策略指导转换为可执行审查引擎，包含类型化关系逻辑规则和原子级提取问题；(2)LLM基于检索的文档证据回答局部问题；(3)符号评估器应用形式化规则检测不合规情况。该方法将策略形式化、局部文档解释和符号合规评估分离处理。

Result: 在公司特定的NDA合规审查任务上实例化并评估了PolicyGuard，该任务要求检查合同条款是否符合组织特定的谈判策略。通过分离策略形式化、局部文档解释和符号合规评估，实现了更明确的文档审查流程。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard通过神经符号结合的方式，成功解决了传统端到端LLM方法在策略导向文档审查中的局限性，使文档审查过程更加显式、可维护且可系统化测试，为组织合规审查提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Policy-grounded document review requires determining whether a target document complies with organization-specific policies, guidelines, or playbooks. While large language models can assist with policy interpretation and document analysis, end-to-end prompting leaves the applied policy logic implicit, making compliance decisions difficult to inspect, update, and test. We present PolicyGuard, a neuro-symbolic framework for policy-grounded document compliance review. PolicyGuard converts organizational policy guidance into an executable review engine consisting of typed relational logic rules and atom-level extraction questions. During review, LLMs answer these local questions using retrieved document evidence, and a symbolic evaluator applies the formal rules to detect non-compliance. We instantiate and evaluate PolicyGuard on company-specific NDA compliance review, where contract clauses must be checked against organization-specific negotiation policies. By separating policy formalization, local document interpretation, and symbolic compliance evaluation, PolicyGuard makes document review more explicit, maintainable, and systematically testable.

</details>


### [75] [AxDafny: Agentic Verified Code Generation in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32007)
*Benjamin Breen,Austin Letson,Borja Requena Pozo,Leopoldo Sarra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AxDafny框架，用于Dafny语言的智能体代码生成与验证修复，并构建了LCB-Pro-Dafny基准测试。实验表明该方法显著提升了验证成功率，且验证成功与运行时测试表现衡量的是代码质量的不同维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dafny中的智能体代码生成问题，即模型需要同时生成可执行代码和验证所需的证明构件（如不变量、断言、终止参数等），以提高形式化验证的成功率。

Method: 提出AxDafny验证器引导修复框架，迭代生成实现、不变量、断言和终止参数；构建LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny基准测试（250道竞赛风格编程问题，配有形式化规范和验证器评估工具）。

Result: 在LCB-Pro-Dafny上，AxDafny显著优于基线GPT-5.5的验证成功率；在DafnyBench上达到92.7%验证成功率，比最强先前证明提示基线提升6.5个百分点。验证成功与运行时测试性能衡量代码的不同方面。

Conclusion: 验证器引导的迭代修复方法能有效提升Dafny代码的验证成功率，验证成功与运行时测试是互补的代码质量评估维度。

Abstract: We study agentic code generation in Dafny, where a model must generate both executable code and the proof artifacts for verification. We present AxDafny, a verifier-guided repair framework that iteratively generates implementations, invariants, assertions, and termination arguments. We also introduce LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny (LCB-Pro-Dafny), a benchmark of 250 competition-style programming problems translated into Dafny with formal specifications and a verifier-based evaluation harness. On LCB-Pro-Dafny, AxDafny substantially improves verification success over baseline GPT-5.5 performance. On DafnyBench, AxDafny achieves 92.7\% verification success, outperforming the strongest previously reported proof-hint baseline by 6.5 percentage points. Lastly, we show that verification success and runtime test performance measure different aspects of generated code.

</details>

### [76] [Governance Gaps in Agent Interoperability Protocols: What MCP, A2A, and ACP Cannot Express](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31498)
*Richard Kang,Yudho Diponegoro*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 五大智能体互操作协议（MCP、A2A、ACP、ANP、ERC-8004）在治理需求方面存在显著缺陷：投票与异议保留功能普遍缺失，协商功能严重不足，协议仅支持任务协调而无法支撑受治理约束的智能体社区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着企业在治理约束下部署异构智能体集群进行集体决策，现有互操作协议能否支持受治理的智能体社区成为一个关键问题。现有协议主要解决身份验证、能力发现、工具访问和消息交换等任务导向型协调问题，但缺乏对社区治理的支持。

Method: 基于组织理论、多智能体系统文献和企业治理标准，构建六维度治理需求分类法（成员资格、协商、投票、异议保留、人工升级、审计/重放）。系统性地对照该分类法分析五个协议规范，将各能力归类为支持、部分支持或缺失三种状态。区分可扩展性缺口（可通过协议扩展机制解决）与结构性缺口（需要新架构层），并基于协议演化速度评估时间敏感性。

Result: 差距矩阵分析显示：投票和异议保留在所有五个协议中均完全缺失；协商功能缺失或至多部分支持；没有任何协议编码了受治理智能体社区所需的完整原语集。智能体社区治理是当前互操作标准之上的缺失架构层，而非协议内部缺失的功能特性。

Conclusion: 当前互操作协议在设计上仅服务于任务导向型协调，无法满足企业级智能体社区的治理需求。需要在现有互操作标准之上构建专门的治理架构层来实现成员管理、集体决策、异议处理和人机协同等治理功能，这为未来协议栈演进指明了方向。

Abstract: Agent interoperability protocols (MCP, A2A, ACP, ANP, and ERC-8004) have rapidly matured to enable identity, capability discovery, tool access, and message exchange between autonomous agents. However, as enterprises deploy heterogeneous agent fleets that must make collective decisions under governance constraints, a question arises: can these protocols support governed agent communities, or only task-oriented coordination? We present a systematic gap analysis applying a six-dimension governance requirements taxonomy (membership, deliberation, voting, dissent preservation, human escalation, and audit/replay) derived from organizational theory, multi-agent systems literature, and enterprise governance standards. We analyze each protocol's specification against this taxonomy, classifying capabilities as Supported, Partial, or Absent. The resulting gap matrix reveals that voting and dissent preservation are universally absent across all five protocols, deliberation is absent or at most partial, and no protocol encodes the full set of primitives required for governed agent communities. We distinguish extensible gaps (addressable through protocol extension mechanisms) from structural gaps (requiring a new architectural layer) and assess time-sensitivity based on observed protocol evolution velocity. The analysis establishes that agent community governance constitutes a missing architectural layer above current interoperability standards, not a missing feature within them.

</details>


### [77] [Holonic Active Distillation for Scalable Multi-Agent Learning in Multi-Sensor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31578)
*Dani Manjah,Tim Bary,Benoît Macq,Stéphane Galland*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于整体多智能体系统（HMAS）的整体主动蒸馏架构，通过CSBAD框架实现传感器网络的可扩展性和适应性，在平衡本地专业化与全局泛化的同时，有效应对传感器的动态加入与离开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器网络的快速扩展在开放环境中带来了可扩展性、适应性和知识迁移的重大挑战，特别是新子系统可能动态加入或离开，需要一种能够应对这些挑战的新型架构。

Method: 提出整体主动蒸馏架构，集成聚类流式主动蒸馏（CSBAD）框架，其中专业化学生模型收集本地数据，从教师模型查询伪标签，并聚类成相似传感器组，形成整体多智能体系统（HMAS）。

Result: 整体组织成功平衡了本地专业化与全局泛化，能够高效适应传感器的离开和重新集成；分析了增量模型更新、系统重组和可扩展性限制之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 整体学习在多传感器系统中具有显著优势，但也识别出模型漂移和长期适应相关的关键挑战，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of sensor-based networks introduces major challenges in scalability, adaptability, and knowledge transfer, especially in open environments where new subsystems can dynamically join or leave. In this work, we propose a Holonic Active Distillation architecture within a Holonic Multi-Agent System (HMAS) to address these issues. Our approach integrates Clustered Stream-Based Active Distillation (CSBAD), a framework in which specialized student models collect local data, query pseudo-labels from teacher models, and cluster into groups of similar sensors.
  Results show that the holonic organization balances local specialization with global generalization, while efficiently adapting to sensor departures and re-integrations. We also analyzed trade-offs among incremental model updates, system reorganization, and scalability limits.
  Our findings highlight the advantages of holonic learning for multi-sensor systems while identifying key challenges related to model drift and long-term adaptation.

</details>


### [78] [ForecastAgentSearch: Towards a Multi-Expert Agent Search System for Geopolitical Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31665)
*Miaomiao Cai,He Chang,Yunshan Ma,See-kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ForecastAgentSearch框架，将地缘政治事件预测建模为多专家智能体搜索问题，通过检索、排序和协调专家智能体来生成带有不确定性感知的预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地缘政治事件预测是一项极具挑战性的任务，需要理解复杂的区域背景、动态事件信号以及不确定的未来结果。大语言模型智能体的最新进展为构建能够利用多种信息源和专家视角进行推理的预测系统提供了新机遇。

Method: 提出ForecastAgentSearch框架，将地缘政治事件预测形式化为多专家智能体搜索问题。系统首先分析任务背景，然后根据区域知识、领域专长、可靠性和互补性搜索并排序相关专家智能体。选定的智能体提供专业化分析，通过多智能体协调机制生成最终预测，并附带解释和不确定性感知。核心设计包括智能体画像、专家检索、排序和多智能体协调。

Result: 提出了一个初步的多专家智能体搜索框架，能够根据预测查询动态检索和排序专家智能体，并协调多个专家分析生成带有解释和不确定性意识的预测结果。讨论了智能体画像、专家检索、排序和多智能体协调等关键设计挑战。

Conclusion: 这项工作为构建可搜索、可靠的基于智能体的预测系统提供了初步框架和方向，为未来研究和系统开发奠定了基础。工作同时概述了未来开发所需的可能评估协议。

Abstract: Geopolitical event forecasting is a challenging task, as it requires understanding complex regional contexts, dynamic event signals, and uncertain future outcomes. Recent advances in large language model agents provide new opportunities for building forecasting systems that can reason with diverse sources and expert perspectives. In this paper, we present \textit{ForecastAgentSearch}, a preliminary framework that formulates geopolitical event forecasting as a multi-expert agent search problem. Given a forecasting query, the system first analyzes the task context, then searches and ranks relevant expert agents based on their regional knowledge, domain expertise, reliability, and complementarity. The selected agents provide specialized analyses, which are further coordinated to generate a final forecast with explanations and uncertainty awareness. We discuss the key design challenges of agent profiling, expert retrieval, ranking, and multi-agent coordination, and outline possible evaluation protocols for future development. This work aims to provide an initial step toward searchable and reliable agent-based forecasting systems.

</details>


### [79] [Analytic Cut in Epistemic Logics with Distributed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31886)
*Ryo Murai,Sizhuo Liu,Katsuhiko Sano*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式知识认知逻辑的相继式演算，证明虽然切消定理不成立，但通过限制切公式可获得分析性切性质和Craig插值定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式知识是多智能体认知逻辑中的重要概念，现有认知逻辑相继式演算中切消定理在K45、KD45和S5系统中失效，需要建立替代的证明论性质。

Method: 基于K45、KD45和S5构建分布式知识认知逻辑的相继式演算系统，采用Takano(2018)的策略，将切公式限制为切规则结论的子公式集合，从而建立分析性切性质。

Result: 证明了所有三个系统（基于K45、KD45和S5的分布式知识逻辑）都具有分析性切性质；作为推论，所有考虑的逻辑都满足Craig插值定理；当允许空群的分布式知识算子时，所有证明论结果仍然有效。

Conclusion: 通过建立分析性切性质，为分布式知识认知逻辑提供了可行的证明论方法，确保了Craig插值定理成立，并将结果推广到包含空群（解释为全局模态）的情形。

Abstract: Distributed knowledge is a notion of group knowledge studied in multi-agent epistemic logic. Semantically, the distributed knowledge of a group is interpreted via an accessibility relation given by the intersection of the epistemic accessibility relations of the agents in that group. This paper investigates sequent calculi for epistemic logics of distributed knowledge based on K45, KD45, and S5. While cut elimination holds in existing sequent calculi for modal logics K45 and KD45, it fails in all the systems mentioned above. Instead, we establish the analytic cut property for all three systems by adapting Takano' s (2018) strategy, which restricts the cut formulas to the set of subformulas of the conclusion of the cut rule. As a corollary, the Craig interpolation theorem holds for all logics considered. We also show that all proof-theoretic results remain valid when the empty group is allowed for the distributed-knowledge operator, in which case the distributed knowledge for the empty group is interpreted as the global modality.

</details>


### [80] [MECoBench: A Systematic Study of Multimodal Agent Collaboration in Embodied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31966)
*Qingyun Liu,Jiwen Zhang,Jingyi Hu,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了MECoBench，一个多模态具身协作基准测试平台，用于评估多模态大语言模型在视觉环境中的协作能力，并通过实验发现了协作收益、通信重要性及鲁棒性提升的三个关键规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）作为具身智能体具有强大潜力，但它们在视觉环境中的协作能力尚未得到充分探索。现有研究缺乏系统性的基准来评估MLLMs在具身任务中的协作表现。

Method: 构建MECoBench多模态具身协作基准测试平台，涵盖多样化的真实世界任务、两种协作结构和三种协作模式，对多种MLLMs进行广泛实验评估。

Result: 通过实验得出三个核心发现：（i）协作通常能提升具身任务完成效果，但收益取决于协作增益与协调复杂度的平衡；（ii）通信对协作增益至关重要，最优协作模式取决于团队规模和模型能力；（iii）协作在噪声先验和探索条件下能提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MECoBench为理解多模态具身协作的机制与局限性提供了系统性测试平台，推动了具身智能协作研究的发展。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have strong potential as embodied agents, but their ability to collaborate in visually grounded environments remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce MECoBench, a multimodal embodied cooperation benchmark with an evaluation platform spanning diverse real-world tasks, two cooperation structures, and three collaboration modes. Through extensive experiments across various MLLMs, we summarize three key findings: (i) Collaboration generally improves embodied task completion, but its benefits depend on balancing collaborative gains against coordination complexity. (ii) Communication is essential to collaboration gains, while the best collaboration mode depends on team size and model capability. (iii) Moreover, collaboration improves robustness under noisy priors and exploration conditions. Generally, MECoBench provides a systematic testbed for understanding the mechanisms and limits of multimodal embodied collaboration. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/q-i-n-g/MECoBench.

</details>

### [81] [SpikON: A Dual-Parallel and Efficient Accelerator for Online Spiking Neural Networks Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30926)
*Peilin Chen,Xiaoxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpikON，首个面向高效可扩展端到端在线监督SNN学习的算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过可学习阈值和权重中心化技术改进算法，并设计双并行引擎的专用加速器，实现了显著的延迟和能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线无监督SNN学习存在训练精度低和可扩展性差的问题，而当前的在线监督学习算法虽然在大规模数据集上表现良好，但其非硬件友好的操作阻碍了高效的边缘部署，因此需要一种算法-硬件协同设计的方法来实现高效的端到端在线监督SNN学习。

Method: 提出了基于时间的可学习阈值和基于时间的缩放权重中心化技术来解决传统算法的低效问题；引入了新型训练数据流和级联计算重用方案，支持前向-后向并发计算和时间步间的时序重用；设计了配备双并行引擎和定制化基于SIMD的SNN核心的专用加速器。

Result: SpikON算法相比基线实现了32.2%的训练延迟降低和35.0%的能耗降低，且不牺牲精度；SpikON协同设计相比边缘Apple M4 GPU实现了7.2倍的训练吞吐量和26.8倍的能效提升，相比类TPU加速器实现了11.5倍的训练吞吐量和15.8倍的能效提升。

Conclusion: SpikON成功证明了算法-硬件协同设计在实现高效可扩展的端到端在线监督SNN学习方面的有效性，为边缘设备上的SNN部署提供了可行的解决方案，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了训练效率和能效。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for energy-efficient brain-inspired computing. However, existing online unsupervised SNN learning suffers from low training accuracy and poor scalability. Although current online supervised learning algorithms perform well on large-scale datasets and networks, the non-hardware-friendly operations hinder efficient edge deployment. In this work, we propose SpikON, the first algorithm-hardware co-design framework for efficient and scalable end-to-end online supervised SNN learning. We first propose the learnable threshold through time and scaled weight centralization through time techniques to address the inefficiency of traditional algorithms. Moreover, to reduce latency and energy consumption, we introduce the novel training dataflow and cascade computation reuse scheme for SNNs that allows concurrent forward-backward computation and temporal reuse across timesteps. We further design the dedicated SNN accelerator with a dual-parallel engine and customized SIMD-based SNN core for efficient end-to-end online learning. Experiments show that the SpikON algorithm achieves 32.2% and 35.0% reductions in training latency and energy consumption over the baseline, without sacrificing accuracy. Moreover, the SpikON co-design achieves 7.2x (11.5x) and 26.8x (15.8x) training throughput (energy efficiency) compared with the edge Apple M4 GPU and TPU-like accelerator, respectively. The code is available at https://github.com/peilin-chen/SpikON.

</details>


### [82] [Dynamic Ultrasound Beamforming Using Left-to-Right Arithmetic Adders on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31490)
*Muhammad Usman,Shujaat Khan,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最高有效位优先（MSDF）算术的加法树架构，相比传统并行加法树，LUT使用量减少2.5倍，动态功耗降低23%，吞吐量提升80%，并支持运行时动态精度调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统并行加法树在延迟求和（DAS）超声波束形成中存在关键路径长、LUT消耗大、小型FPGA时序收敛困难等问题，无法满足实时成像流水线对能耗、吞吐量和面积的综合需求。

Method: 设计了一种基于左到右（LR）或最高有效位优先（MSDF）算术的加法树架构，该架构无需等待全精度完成即可生成高质量波束形成图像，通过简单停止计算时钟实现运行时精度选择，从而实现动态精度控制。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq XC7Z010 FPGA上实现并评估64通道超声数据集的DAS波束形成：LUT使用量比最小传统加法树减少2.5倍，满足时序约束，动态功耗比最高效传统基线低23%，同面积复制可实现15个并行实例，达到67 FPS，吞吐量比最佳传统设计高80%。

Conclusion: 所提出的MSDF加法树架构有效解决了传统加法树的资源消耗和时序问题，实现了质量-能耗可扩展性，为FPGA上的实时超声成像提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Adder trees are the computational backbone of delay-and-sum (DAS) ultrasound beamforming, where their implementation directly determines the energy, throughput, and area of a real-time imaging pipeline. Conventional parallel adder trees perform full-precision combinational reduction on every sample, leading to wide critical paths, high LUT consumption, and timing failures on small FPGA devices. This paper presents an alternative adder tree architecture based on \emph{left-to-right (LR)} or \emph{most significant digit first (MSDF) arithmetic}. We implement the proposed and conventional adder trees on a Xilinx Zynq XC7Z010 FPGA and evaluate them for DAS beamforming of a 64-channel ultrasound dataset. The proposed design uses 2.5$\times$ fewer LUTs than the smallest conventional tree, successfully meets the timing constraint, and consumes 23\% less dynamic power than the most efficient conventional baseline. A key advantage of the proposed MSDF adder tree is that it can generate high-quality beamformed images without waiting for full-precision completion. This naturally enables dynamic precision at runtime with negligible control overhead, since precision selection is achieved simply by stopping the computation clock after the desired number of cycles. Such quality--energy scalability is fundamentally unavailable in conventional fixed-cycle adder trees. Iso-area replication enables up to 15 parallel instances on the XC7Z010, achieving 67 FPS, which is 80\% higher throughput than the best conventional design.

</details>


### [83] [MINT: Dynamic-Precision CNN Inference with MSDF Digit-Serial Arithmetic on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31514)
*Muhammad Usman,Malik Zohaib Nisar,Florian Aschauer,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MINT，一种基于从左到右（LR）算术的动态精度CNN推理加速器，通过提前终止计算实现精度与效率的平衡，在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现了最高能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN推理采用固定精度计算，存在计算冗余。LR算术能够以最高有效位优先的方式计算，提前暴露有用的部分结果，从而在达到所需精度后及时终止计算，降低不必要的计算开销。

Method: 提出MINT加速器，核心包括：1）MSDF串并内积单元，采用冗余有符号数表示法计算每个卷积窗口；2）预算约束的贪心搜索策略，对VGG-16和ResNet-18的所有卷积层从INT2到INT7进行性能分析，选择每层最低精度，同时将总精度损失控制在INT8基准的2%以内。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq-7020（200MHz）上综合，VGG-16和ResNet-18的平均位数分别为5.64和6.04。VGG-16实现19.86 GOPS吞吐量和29.51 GOPS/W能效，ResNet-18实现18.86 GOPS吞吐量和26.40 GOPS/W能效。相比INT8基准，吞吐量分别提高32.6%和26.0%，能效分别提高82.10%和62.90%，精度仅下降1.81%和1.96%。在Zynq-7020平台的VGG-16和ResNet-18设计中达到最高能效。

Conclusion: MINT通过LR算术实现动态精度计算，在保持接近INT8精度的同时显著提升了吞吐量和能效，为CNN推理加速提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present MINT, a dynamic-precision CNN inference accelerator based on left-to-right (LR) arithmetic. LR arithmetic computes in most-significant-digit-first manner and exposes useful partial results early so that the computation can be terminated once the desired precision is achieved. At the core, there is a MSDF serial-parallel inner-product unit, which uses redundant signed-digit representation to compute each convolution window. A budget-constrained greedy search profiles all convolution layers from INT2 to INT7 and selects the lowest precision per layer while constraining total accuracy loss to within 2\% of the INT8 baseline for VGG-16 and ResNet-18 networks. The design is synthesized on a Xilinx Zynq-7020 at \SI{200}{\mega\hertz}, and uses 5.64 average bits for VGG-16 and 6.04 for ResNet-18, while achieving 19.86 GOPS and 29.51 GOPS/W on VGG-16, and 18.86 GOPS and 26.40 GOPS/W on ResNet-18. This corresponds to 32.6\% and 26.0\% higher throughput and 82.10\% and 62.90\% higher energy efficiency than INT8 with only 1.81\% and 1.96\% drops relative to the INT8 baseline. Compared with representative prior FPGA CNN accelerators considered in this study, MINT delivers the highest energy efficiency among the listed VGG-16 and ResNet-18 designs on Zynq-7020 platform.

</details>


### [84] [PEERS: A Parallel and Exact Effective Resistance Solver via Implicit Inversion and Augmented Symbolic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31535)
*Baiyu Chen,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了PEERS，一种并行精确有效电阻求解器，解决了EDA签核中大规模电网分析的精度-内存瓶颈，实现了百万节点工业图18.8秒内处理完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度有效电阻计算是EDA签核的核心，但在大规模电网分析、谱稀疏化和电路可靠性中存在严重瓶颈。现有方法面临"精度-内存困境"：近似方法缺乏工业签核所需的严格精度，而精确方法要么存在冗余查询开销，要么触发O(n²)内存爆炸。

Method: 提出PEERS求解器，采用基于Cholesky因子的隐式逆计算模型。通过集成状态继承的增强深度优先搜索（DFS）和动态查询更新机制，消除数值冗余并在单次并行扫描中评估所有边电阻查询。提供严格的Work-Span分析。

Result: 对于满足O(n^α)分隔定理的图，PEERS实现O(n^α)的理论最优并行跨度，同时严格保持O(nnz(L))空间复杂度。在工业基准测试上，相同内存约束下相比最先进并行求解器平均加速83.3倍。百万节点工业图处理仅需18.8秒，扩展至1700万节点不到1小时。

Conclusion: PEERS为多百万门设计中的精确全边电阻分析提供了首个计算可行方案，突破了大规模电网分析中精度与效率的根本矛盾，具有重要的工业应用价值。

Abstract: High-precision effective resistance computation is a cornerstone of Electronic Design Automation (EDA) sign-off, yet it remains a fundamental bottleneck in large-scale power grid analysis, spectral sparsification, and circuit reliability. Existing approaches face a prohibitive "precision-memory impasse": approximate methods lack the stringent accuracy required for high-stakes industrial sign-off, while exact methods either suffer from redundant query overheads or trigger $O(n^2)$ memory explosions. To resolve this, we propose PEERS, a Parallel and Exact Effective Resistance Solver powered by an implicit inverse computing model of the Cholesky factor. By integrating a state-inherited augmented depth-first search (DFS) with a dynamic query update mechanism, PEERS eliminates numerical redundancy and evaluates all-edge resistance queries in a single parallel sweep. We provide a rigorous Work-Span analysis, proving that for graphs satisfying an $O(n^α)$ separator theorem, PEERS achieves a theoretically optimal parallel span of $O(n^α)$ while strictly maintaining $O(nnz(L))$ space complexity. Numerical evaluations on industrial benchmarks demonstrate that PEERS achieves an average speedup of 83.3x over state-of-the-art parallel solvers under identical memory constraints. Notably, PEERS processes a 1-million-node industrial graph in just 18.8 seconds and scales to 17 million nodes in under an hour, providing the first computationally feasible path for exact all-edge resistance analysis in multi-million-gate designs.

</details>


### [85] [In-situ Indexing via Memristive Content-Addressable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31554)
*Bing Wu,Xueliang Wei,Shiyi Song,Yibo Liu,Jinpeng Liu,Wei Tong,Hao Tong,Yuchong Hu,Dan Feng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PATH，一种利用PIM（Processing-in-Memory）技术实现的原位索引架构，通过将索引操作转化为高度并行的内容寻址存储操作，显著提升了哈希索引的吞吐量（4.7-7.8倍）、降低了尾延迟（>14.5倍）并减少了内存访问次数（>61.4%），有效突破了内存墙对数据索引的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然PIM技术已被证明能够克服“内存墙”问题，但数据索引同样严重受限于内存墙，目前尚不清楚如何使索引有效地利用PIM的独特能力。研究者希望弥合这一差距，探索索引如何从PIM的大规模并行性和固有的数据移动能力中获益。

Method: 提出PATH原位索引架构，将基本索引操作（插入、搜索、更新、删除）重构为在存储阵列内直接执行的高度并行原位内容寻址存储操作。以哈希索引为典型实例，采用超大逻辑桶和内存内移动技术，打破了传统哈希方案中内存访问、负载因子和处理延迟之间的内在权衡。

Result: 与最先进的方案相比，PATH在插入操作上实现了4.7-7.8倍的吞吐量提升，尾延迟降低超过14.5倍，内存访问次数减少超过61.4%。通过超大逻辑桶和内存内移动，实际上消除了哈希冲突解决的代价，并显著降低了调整大小（resizing）的开销。

Conclusion: PATH成功地将PIM技术应用于数据索引领域，通过原位并行操作有效解决了传统哈希索引的关键瓶颈问题，为下一代以数据为中心的计算提供了可扩展的基础架构。该研究证明了PIM在索引领域的巨大潜力，为未来数据库系统和存储引擎的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) is a proven paradigm for overcoming the ``memory wall". However, while data indexing is severely bottlenecked by this same wall, it remains unclear how indexing can effectively benefit from PIM's unique capabilities. We present PATH, an in-situ indexing architecture that bridges this gap by leveraging the massive parallelism and inherent data-movement of PIMs. Specifically, we first reformulate the fundamental indexing operations, namely Insert, Search, Update, and Delete, into highly parallel in-situ content-addressable memory operations executed directly within memory arrays. Taking hash indexes as a typical case, we elaborate how PATH breaks the inherent trade-off among memory accesses, load factor, and process latency in conventional hashing schemes. By adopting ultra-large logical buckets and in-memory moving, PATH virtually eliminates the cost of hash collision resolution and significantly reduces resizing overhead. Compared with state-of-the-art schemes, PATH achieves $4.7-7.8\times$ higher throughput, $>14.5\times$ lower tail latency, and $>61.4\%$ fewer memory accesses under insertions, laying a scalable foundation for next-generation data-centric computing.

</details>


### [86] [FlexViT: A Flexible FPGA-based Accelerator for Edge Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31938)
*Hubert Dymarkowski,Xingjian Fu,Rappy Saha,Jude Haris,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlexViT，一种基于FPGA的可重配置加速器，通过软硬件协同设计和运行时数据流重配置，实现了混合ViT模型的高效边缘推理，相比纯CPU执行获得最高1.40倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer (ViT) 模型在边缘平台部署面临巨大挑战：计算需求高，且现代混合ViT模型架构异构性强（同时包含全连接层和卷积层），导致张量形状变化显著，对FPGA加速器的灵活性和效率提出了更高要求。

Method: 基于SECDA-TFLite框架构建软硬件协同设计：(1) 采用统一的高吞吐INT8 GEMM引擎，通过运行时im2col变换将全连接和卷积层映射到同一引擎；(2) 提出双模式数据流，通过运行时重配置计算阵列在输入复用和权重复用之间动态切换；(3) 引入深度优先分块策略，单次遍历完成累加，消除片外部分和传输并降低存储带宽需求。

Result: 在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上实现并评估多个代表性ViT模型，加速器执行层获得最高2.74倍加速，端到端速度提升最高达1.40倍（相比纯CPU执行）。

Conclusion: FlexViT通过统一的GEMM引擎、可重配置数据流和深度优先分块策略，有效解决了混合ViT模型在资源受限边缘设备上的高效部署问题，为异构神经网络架构的FPGA加速提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying Vision Transformer (ViT) models on edge platforms remains challenging due to their high computational demands and the architectural heterogeneity of modern hybrid ViT models, which incorporate both fully connected and convolutional layers. This heterogeneity leads to significant variation in tensor shapes, requiring flexible and efficient FPGA-based acceleration. In this paper, we present FlexViT, a reconfigurable FPGA accelerator for efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge devices. Built on the SECDA-TFLite framework, FlexViT employs a hardware-software co-design approach that maps both fully connected and convolutional layers onto a unified high-throughput INT8 GEMM engine using a runtime im2col transformation. To efficiently support diverse layer configurations, we propose a dual-mode dataflow that dynamically switches between input and weight reuse by reconfiguring the compute array at runtime. We further introduce a depth-first tiling strategy that completes accumulation in a single pass, eliminating off-chip partial-sum transfers and reducing memory bandwidth requirements. We implement FlexViT on a PYNQ-Z2 FPGA and evaluate it across a representative set of ViT models. FlexViT achieves up to 2.74x speedup on accelerator-executed layers, translating into up to 1.40x end-to-end speedup compared to CPU-only execution. The code is available at: https://github.com/gicLAB/FlexViT

</details>

### [87] [LUMOS: A Semantic Operating-System Layer for Accessibility-Grounded AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30697)
*Yogeswar Reddy Thota*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出了LUMOS，一个连接AI智能体与操作系统的语义交互层，通过将原生可访问性元数据转换为机器可读的语义蓝图，减少对截图的依赖，降低token成本和视觉歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前操作系统的界面针对人类用户优化（像素、图标、窗口等），而非AI智能体。AI智能体需要紧凑的语义状态、基于事实的动作和可靠反馈。现有方法依赖截图、OCR和视觉裁剪，导致token成本高、视觉歧义、延迟和坐标不确定性等问题。

Method: 构建LUMOS语义交互层：1) 将原生可访问性元数据和浏览器UI结构转换为具有稳定标识符、角色、名称、值、边界和动作可供性的机器可读语义蓝图；2) 通过操作系统自动化API支持实时语义指针定位；3) LLM通过基于可访问性的观察-动作循环，使用受限的可见UI原语而非特定应用脚本执行操作。

Result: LUMOS成功实现了AI智能体与操作系统的语义级交互，通过操作系统已有的语义结构减少了对截图的依赖，降低了token成本、视觉歧义、延迟和坐标不确定性问题。

Conclusion: 研究为AI原生操作系统和机器可读交互层的发展指明了方向，表明在操作系统已提供语义结构的情况下，可以减少对视觉智能体的依赖。

Abstract: Current operating systems expose interfaces optimized for human users but not for AI agents. Humans benefit from pixels, icons, windows, visual grouping, mouse movement, and keyboard shortcuts; AI agents instead need compact semantic state, grounded actions, and reliable feedback. As a result, many computer-use agents are forced to interpret screenshots, OCR output, and visual crops, introducing high token costs, visual ambiguity, latency, and coordinate uncertainty. This paper introduces LUMOS (Language Model Unified Machine-Readable Operating-System Semantics), a semantic interaction layer between AI agents and operating systems. LUMOS converts native accessibility metadata and browser UI structures into machine readable semantic blueprints with stable identifiers, roles, names, values, bounds, and action affordances. It also supports live semantic pointer grounding by querying the UI element under or near the cursor through operating-system automation APIs. An LLM then acts through an accessibility grounded observe act loop using constrained visible-UI primitives rather than application-specific scripts. LUMOS does not claim to replace visual agents; instead, it reduces dependence on screenshots when operating systems already provide semantic structure. These results suggest a path toward AI-native operating systems and machine-readable interaction layers.

</details>


### [88] [Ensuring Deterministic Timing in a Federated GNSS Correction Pipeline with Lingua Franca](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31415)
*Tejeswini Jayaramareddy,Hoeseok Yang,Hokeun Kim*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文利用Lingua Franca的联邦化应用和显式逻辑时间语义，成功将原本被视为异步难分析的嵌入式GNSS数据流水线建模为确定性时序结构，并通过实验验证了其可重现性和可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合硬件中断、缓冲和分布式通信的嵌入式系统通常被视为本质上异步且难以分析，研究者希望证明此类系统在使用显式逻辑时间语义建模时可以表现出确定性的时序结构。

Method: 将端到端的GNSS校正数据流水线实现为一个联邦化的Lingua Franca (LF)应用程序，将其分解为具有显式时间语义的响应器，包括时间触发的GNSS接收器、基于波特率和FIFO缓冲区特性的UART中断流、周期性转发任务以及带有抖动监控的下游处理。

Result: 联邦执行和运行时日志验证了分析推导出的确定性时序结构，包括中断节律、环形缓冲区演化、打包行为以及物理-逻辑时间抖动，从而产生了一个可重现且可预测的时序轮廓。

Conclusion: 通过使用Lingua Franca和显式逻辑时间语义对嵌入式系统进行建模，可以将看似异步复杂的系统转化为具有确定性时序结构的系统，提高了系统的可分析性和可预测性。

Abstract: Embedded systems that combine hardware interrupts, buffering, and distributed communication are often perceived as inherently asynchronous and difficult to analyze. However, such systems can exhibit a deterministic timing structure when modeled using explicit logical-time semantics.
  This paper presents a Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) correction-data pipeline implemented as a federated Lingua Franca (LF) application. The federated LF program decomposes the end-to-end pipeline into reactors with explicit time semantics, including a time-triggered GNSS receiver, a UART interrupt stream derived from baud rate and First-In First-Out (FIFO) buffer characteristics, a periodic forwarding task, and downstream processing with jitter monitoring.
  Federated execution and runtime logs validate the analytically derived deterministic timing structure-including interrupt cadence, ring-buffer evolution, packetization behavior, and physical--logical jitter-yielding a reproducible and predictable timing profile.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Joint discovery of governing partial differential equations from multi-source datasets by competitive optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30699)
*Hao Xu,Siyu Lou,Yuntian Chen,Dongxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCO-PDE框架，通过竞争优化机制融合多源数据集，实现从有限观测中高效发现共享偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的方程发现方法通常在单一数据集上运行，当观测数据受限时性能受限。实际上，同一物理系统往往存在多个数据集（仅初始条件或边界配置不同），融合这些多源数据可提升发现性能。

Method: 提出MCO-PDE框架：(1)为每个数据源训练独立的神经代理模型；(2)采用软竞争加权机制动态评估数据集可信度并聚合全局共识系数；(3)结合遗传算法进行结构搜索，同时识别控制方程的函数形式和参数。

Result: 在7个案例中，每个数据集仅需50个观测点即可高精度恢复经典方程；成功处理具有不规则边界和异质系数的二维/三维域；成功从真实造波水槽实验中提取物理规律。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过异质数据融合实现自动化科学发现建立了有前景的途径，证明了多源数据融合在方程发现任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Discovering governing equations directly from observational data is a key step towards interpretable scientific machine learning. Current data-driven approaches typically operate on a single dataset, inherently limiting their performance when faced with restricted observations. In practice, multiple datasets are often available for the same physical system, distinguished only by distinct initial conditions or boundary configurations. Here, we present a competitive optimization framework designed to discover shared partial differential equations (PDEs) from multi-source datasets, termed MCO-PDE. The framework first trains independent neural surrogates for each data source, and then employs a soft-competitive weighting mechanism to dynamically assess dataset credibility and aggregate a consensus global coefficient. Integrated with a genetic algorithm for structural search, this approach simultaneously identifies the functional forms and parameters of the governing laws. We demonstrate that fusing as few as 50 observations per dataset across seven cases recovers canonical equations with high accuracy. The framework inherently handles two- and three-dimensional domains characterized by irregular boundaries and heterogeneous coefficients, and successfully extracts physically meaningful laws from real-world wave-tank experiments. Overall, this work establishes a promising route for automated scientific discovery via heterogeneous data fusion.

</details>


### [90] [Accelerometry-Derived Digital Biomarkers for Cardiometabolic Risk: A Population-Representative Tabular Benchmark with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30702)
*Federico Felizzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了基于NHANES数据的加速度计心脏代谢基准，用于评估表格学习方法在临床预测中的表现和公平性。TabPFN v2表现最佳，但预测区间在特定人口统计学亚组中存在覆盖率不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准数据集无法反映真实临床环境中的复杂特性，如复杂调查抽样、人口统计学过采样和亚组公平性。结构化表格数据在临床医学中占主导地位，需要能够体现这些真实世界属性的评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含1,381名成年人的NHANES加速度计心脏代谢基准数据集（2003-2006年），包含髋部佩戴的加速度计数据、空腹实验室生物标志物、饮食摄入和人体测量数据。评估了三种表格学习方法：岭回归、XGBoost和基础模型TabPFN v2，预测糖化血红蛋白（HbA1c）、空腹甘油三酯和C反应蛋白（CRP）。应用分裂保形预测生成无分布90%预测区间，并评估性别和种族/族裔亚组的人口统计学覆盖率公平性。

Result: TabPFN v2取得最佳整体性能（HbA1c R²=0.156，CRP R²=0.383），甘油三酯基本不可预测（R² < 0.05），这与已知的遗传主导性一致。CRP和HbA1c的边际覆盖率符合90%目标，但甘油三酯低于目标。在亚组层面观察到局部覆盖率不足（如墨西哥裔美国参与者的HbA1c预测），揭示了边际保证与临床公平性所需的条件覆盖率之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该基准数据集成功揭示了现有预测方法在真实临床数据上的性能局限性及公平性问题，特别是在亚组水平的覆盖率差异，为临床应用的公平性评估提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Structured tabular data dominates clinical medicine, yet existing benchmarks fail to reflect real-world properties like complex survey sampling, demographic oversampling, and subgroup fairness. We introduce the NHANES Accelerometry Cardiometabolic Benchmark, derived from NHANES 2003-2006, comprising 1,381 adults with hip-worn accelerometry, fasting laboratory biomarkers, dietary intake, and anthropometrics. We evaluate three tabular learning methods -- ridge regression, XGBoost, and the foundation model TabPFN v2 -- to predict glycated haemoglobin (HbA1c), fasting triglycerides, and C-reactive protein (CRP) from activity phenotypes and lifestyle covariates. TabPFN v2 achieves the best overall performance (HbA1c R^2=0.156, CRP R^2=0.383), while triglycerides remain largely unpredictable (R^2 < 0.05), consistent with known genetic dominance. We apply split conformal prediction to generate distribution-free 90% prediction intervals and evaluate demographic coverage equity across sex and race/ethnicity subgroups. Marginal coverage aligns with the 90% target for CRP and HbA1c but falls below for triglycerides. At the subgroup level, we observe localized undercoverage (e.g., HbA1c for Mexican American participants), illustrating the gap between marginal guarantees and the conditional coverage required for clinical fairness. Code and data are at https://github.com/felizzi/nhanes-accel-cardiometabolic-benchmark.

</details>


### [91] [From Search to Synthesis: Training LLMs as Zero-Shot Workflow Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30704)
*Gan Luo,Zihan Qin,Bin Dong,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaFlow是一个基于元学习的自动工作流生成框架，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+强化学习）生成任务级别的可复用工作流，在问答、代码生成和数学推理任务上达到SOTA性能并展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然任务表现优异，但其实例级解决方案缺乏结构一致性。任务级工作流能提供跨实例的鲁棒性、可解释的调试轨迹和可复用性，但人工设计需要大量专业知识。现有自动生成方法要么无法学习任务级模式，要么无法泛化到训练配置之外。

Method: MetaFlow将工作流生成建模为元学习问题：给定任务和算子集合，学习组合解决策略。采用两阶段训练：(1)在合成工作流数据上进行监督微调；(2)使用可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)，通过任务中问题实例的执行反馈提升端到端成功率。

Result: 在问答、代码生成和数学推理基准测试中，MetaFlow在域内任务上以单次推理达到SOTA基线性能，同时在域外任务和新算子集合上展现出显著的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaFlow成功实现了任务级工作流的自动生成，既保证了训练任务的有效性，又具备对未训练任务和新算子集合的强泛化能力，为大语言模型的结构化部署提供了可行的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel across a wide range of tasks, yet their instance-specific solutions often lack the structural consistency needed for reliable deployment. Workflows that encode recurring algorithmic patterns at the task level provide a principled framework, offering robustness across instance variations, interpretable traces for debugging, and reusability across problem instances. However, manually designing such workflows requires significant expertise and effort, limiting their broader application. While automatic workflow generation could address this bottleneck, existing methods either produce instance-specific solutions without learning task-level patterns, or cannot generalize beyond their training configurations. We present MetaFlow, which casts workflow generation as a meta-learning problem: given a task and an operator set, the model learns to compose solution strategies. MetaFlow trains in two stages: supervised fine-tuning on synthetic workflow data, followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) that uses execution feedback across problem instances in the task to improve end-to-end success. The resulting model produces effective workflows for trained tasks and exhibits strong generalization to untrained tasks and novel operator sets. Across benchmarks in question answering, code generation, and mathematical reasoning, MetaFlow achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines on in-domain tasks with single inference, while demonstrating remarkable zero-shot generalization capabilities on out-of-domain tasks and operator sets.

</details>


### [92] [Why Do Few-Step Text Latents Fail When Image Latents Work? Non-Commitment at Sharp Categorical Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30705)
*Zhongyao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了确定性少步生成在连续文本潜变量上失败的根本原因是几何约束：平滑确定性映射无法在离散分类输出前解析分支选择。提出了两种诊断指标和两种可规避此限制的机制，并建立了准确率-深度-刚度的理论权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性少步生成方法在连续图像潜变量上成功，但在连续文本潜变量上会退化为不连贯文本。本文旨在探明这一失败的根源，判断其是训练或缩放不足导致，还是存在更本质的几何约束。

Method: 1. 理论证明：在重叠机制下，后验均值终端步在决策边界附近以潜在质量速率翻转token（定理3）；在分离机制下建立锐利传输定律（定理5-7、17）。2. 提出两个诊断指标：DABI（读出锐度）和CCI（类别承诺度）。3. 在已发布的检查点上测量四个独立构建的连续文本解码器与图像解码器进行对比。

Result: 1. 文本解码器对边界对齐扰动的放大倍数（DABI）远超范数匹配的各向同性扰动（从$5\times10^{2}$到$>10^{5}$），而图像解码器DABI≈1。2. 两种机制可规避连续约束：（a）类别承诺（自回归解码器）；（b）随机重注入（同一模型ODE在K=4时PPL=294 vs SDE=50）。3. 维数相图：分离M个模式所需确定性刚度在潜变量维度Ω(log M)时增长为Θ(√log M)，深度B的层级使峰值减小√B倍。
