RC-TGAN), a temporal extension of the RC-TGAN framework, equipped with a novel integrated loss function based on the \textit{Spectral Envelope Theory}. This differentiable loss allows the generator to directly optimize the preservation of latent periodic structures via backpropagation. While spectral envelope theory is inherently designed for categorical sequences, we extend this frequency-domain regularization to continuous time series by employing a Variational Gaussian Mixture Model (VGM) discretization strategy. To establish a mathematically rigorous evaluation standard, we simulate categorical time series governed by a parameter $α$, with exactly known theoretical spectral envelopes. Integrating these dynamic sequences into the child tables of a relational database yields a robust ground-truth benchmark for evaluating the frequency-domain fidelity of our generative framework. Furthermore, we address the lack of robust evaluation standards for relational time series by proposing two new metrics: Spectral Density Divergence and Spectral Envelope Divergence. Experimental results on real-world datasets, as well as our simulated benchmarks, demonstrate that our end-to-end approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in reproducing cyclic patterns and long-term seasonality across both categorical and continuous features.

</details>


### [164] [Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioning for Robust Cohesive Zone Model Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31921)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了接口感知神经牛顿预处理器(IA-NNP)，用于解决内聚力模型(CZM)在隐式准静态有限元分析中因界面软化导致的数值收敛困难问题。该方法将人工NR修改泛化为学习的、状态依赖的界面修正，在保持原始牵引-分离定律不变的情况下，显著提高了困难增量的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内聚力模型(CZMs)广泛用于航空航天复合材料结构的界面断裂、分层、粘接失效和纤维-基体脱粘模拟。然而在隐式准静态有限元分析中，界面软化可能导致负界面切线、解跳跃和牛顿盆地不匹配等问题，使前一个收敛状态成为下一个增量的糟糕初始猜测。这会导致迭代停滞、错误分支收敛或重复步长缩减。现有补救措施（粘性正则化、路径跟踪、动态松弛和人工NR修改）要么改变有效响应，要么增加成本，或依赖手工制定的界面规则，存在明显局限性。

Method: 本文提出接口感知神经牛顿预处理器(IA-NNP)，将人工NR修改重构为基于规则的界面提升，并将其推广为学习的、状态依赖的界面修正方法。该方法仅作用于活动界面变量，保持原始牵引-分离定律、残差组装、切线评估、历史更新和耗散检查不变。开发了两种实现：(1) IA-NNP-Init：用于学习初始猜测提升；(2) IA-NNP-NL：用于迭代级非线性右预调节。界面图特征编码包括张开度、牵引力、切线、损伤/历史变量、模式混合度、残差和邻近状态。修正具有有界性、置信门控特性，仅通过原始CZM牛顿求解接受，并具有根等价性质——改变收敛路径但不改变离散CZM解集。

Result: 在水平、圆形、双界面和活动前沿基准测试中，IA-NNP相比标准NR和人工NR修改展现出显著改进：困难增量收敛性提升、分支恢复能力增强、失败次数减少。更重要的是，该方法成功保持了力-位移响应不变，验证了其在不改变物理响应的前提下提高数值稳定性的能力。根等价性质的证明确保了解的正确性。

Conclusion: IA-NNP为CZM模拟中的数值收敛困难提供了一个有效的学习增强解决方案。该方法通过智能界面修正显著提高了计算鲁棒性，同时完全保持了原始模型的物理特性。这为航空航天复合材料结构的界面失效分析提供了一种可靠、高效的数值方法，避免了现有方法对物理响应的干扰或计算成本的增加。

Abstract: Cohesive Zone Models (CZMs) are widely used to simulate interface fracture, delamination, adhesive failure, and fiber--matrix debonding in aerospace composite structures. In implicit quasi-static finite element analyses, cohesive softening may introduce negative interface tangents, solution jumps, and Newton-basin mismatch, so the previous converged state can become a poor initial guess for the next increment. This may lead to stagnation, wrong-branch convergence, or repeated step cuts. Existing remedies, including viscous regularization, path following, dynamic relaxation, and manual Newton--Raphson (NR) modification, either alter the effective response, increase cost, or rely on hand-crafted interface rules. This work proposes an Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioner (IA-NNP) for difficult CZM increments. IA-NNP recasts manual NR modification as rule-based interface lifting and generalizes it into a learned, state-dependent interface correction. The method acts only on active interface variables and preserves the original traction--separation law, residual assembly, tangent evaluation, history update, and dissipation checks. Two realizations are developed: IA-NNP-Init for learned initial-guess lifting and IA-NNP-NL for iteration-level nonlinear right preconditioning. Interface graph features encode opening, traction, tangent, damage/history variables, mode mixity, residuals, and neighboring states. The correction is bounded, confidence-gated, and accepted only through the original CZM Newton solve. A root-equivalence property shows that IA-NNP changes the path to convergence but not the discrete CZM solution set. Tests on horizontal, circular, two-interface, and active-front benchmarks show improved difficult-increment convergence, better branch recovery, and fewer failures than standard NR and manual NR modification, while preserving the force--displacement response.

</details>


### [165] [Making Sense of Touch from the Child's View for Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31943)
*Max Whitton,Zecheng Wang,Puchen Liu,Quang Tuan Truong,Shengao Wang,Manaswi Yadamreddy,Oktay Ozel,Visista Jayanti,Saniya Sekhon,Hanna Samuel Tadesse,Lawrence Miao,Junjie Wang,Jiasen Lu,Chen Yu,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了婴儿触觉事件数据集（264k片段），通过预训练模型初步验证了触觉在婴儿视觉概念学习中的作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究触觉是否为婴儿视觉概念学习的机制之一，并量化触觉在视觉学习中的重要性和依赖程度。

Method: 提出结构化编码系统标注婴儿中心触觉事件，构建包含264k个两秒触觉事件片段的数据集，并基于此数据集预训练发展性基础模型。

Result: 预训练模型揭示了触觉对婴儿学习的性质，为理解触觉在视觉学习中的作用提供了有价值的洞察。

Conclusion: 触觉是婴儿视觉概念学习的重要机制，通过结构化编码系统和大规模数据集能够量化研究触觉在认知发展中的作用。

Abstract: Is the sense of touch a mechanism for human babies' learning of visual concepts? If so, can we quantify its importance, and to what extent do babies rely on their sense of touch for visual learning? To approach these questions in a principled way, we propose a structured coding system for baby-centric touch events, yielding a dataset of 264k two-second clips of touch events coded according to this system. Using this dataset, we pretrain developmentally grounded models that reveal promising insights into the nature of baby learning from touch.

</details>


### [166] [Signed-Permutation Coordinate Transport for RMSNorm Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31963)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示RMSNorm模型的残差流存在符号-置换规范(B_d)，仅用置换对齐(S_d)是不完整的。提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配，通过规范保真传输显著提升跨检查点坐标恢复率(91.1% vs 60.3%)，修复SAE重建、情感引导等下游任务中的工具失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM工作流中跨检查点移动坐标索引对象（如转向向量、稀疏自编码器等）需要固定模型的残差流规范。现有置换对齐方法在RMSNorm模型中存在对称性不完整问题，导致工具迁移和可解释性声明失效。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明LayerNorm残差流具有置换规范S_d，而RMSNorm具有符号-置换规范B_d=S_d⋉{±1}^d；2. 提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配算法，消除原始符号相关匹配的结构性精度上限；3. 构建规范保真传输方法：沿相同基座微调轨迹组合局部B_d规范。

Result: 1. 坐标恢复：1500步时跨运行坐标恢复率达91.1%（端点匹配仅60.3%）；2. 工具迁移：TinyLlama SAE重建NMSE为0.004（S_d下为1.08）；Qwen情感引导保留95.8%效果（S_d仅17.2%）；拒绝引导在S_d下符号反转；3. 训练状态：符号传输的AdamW状态保持恢复轨迹，仅置换状态偏离；4. 规范审计显示索引级可解释性声明仅在显式规范下可复现。

Conclusion: 坐标索引对象迁移需要显式规范固定。RMSNorm模型必须使用符号-置换规范B_d而非仅置换S_d。规范保真传输显著优于函数级合并，对SAE、转向向量、模型合并和训练状态恢复均有效。索引级可解释性声明必须相对于显式规范才能复现。

Abstract: Modern LLM workflows move coordinate-indexed objects across checkpoints: steering vectors, sparse autoencoders, top-$k$ neuron sets, attribution lists, and merge alignments. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \ltimes \{\pm 1\}^d$. Permutation-only alignment is therefore symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm models. We introduce sign-marginalized Hungarian matching and prove a sharp failure mode: with decorrelated coordinates, raw signed-correlation matching has a structural permutation-accuracy ceiling at the positive-sign fraction of the true gauge, which sign-marginalization removes. We then make coordinate-preserving transport, not function-level merging, the primary object: composing saved-checkpoint local $B_d$ gauges along same-base fine-tuning trajectories recovers 91.1% of cross-run coordinates at 1500 steps versus 60.3% for endpoint matching, and the gain is not explained by merely routing through the base. The recovered gauge transfers tools that permutation-only alignment breaks: TinyLlama SAE reconstruction has NMSE 0.004 under $B_d$ versus 1.08 under $S_d$; Qwen sentiment steering preserves 95.8% of its effect versus 17.2%; refusal steering reverses sign under $S_d$; coordinate-preserving merges behave the same way. The same covariance governs stateful training: signed transport of AdamW state preserves the resumed trajectory, while permutation-only state follows a different one from a functionally identical checkpoint. Finally, gauge-sweep audits show index-level interpretability claims are reproducible only relative to an explicit gauge.

</details>


### [167] [Amplifying Membership Signal Through Chained Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31991)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MADreMIA框架，通过迭代再生轨迹增强生成模型的成员推断攻击(MIA)和数据集推理(DI)，在多种模型和模态上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型生成模型存在训练数据记忆化问题，使得样本验证对隐私审计和版权执法至关重要。然而现有MIA和DI攻击依赖单次生成，信号微弱且跨模态敏感性有限，且阴影模型训练对大型生成模型不可行。

Method: 受模型自噬失调(MAD)启发，提出MADreMIA框架。该框架利用链式生成跨不同模态的迭代轨迹，每次输出作为下一次输入，无需阴影模型训练。通过观察记忆化训练样本在迭代再生过程中表现出更高连贯性和更慢退化来提取成员证据。

Result: 实验证明记忆化的训练样本在迭代再生过程中比非成员生成表现出显著更高的连贯性和更慢的退化速度。MADreMIA在多种模型家族和模态上提供更丰富的信号，在低误报率(FPR)下改善成员证据质量，涵盖自回归图像模型、扩散模型和语言模型，并在音频模型上展示初步潜力。

Conclusion: MADreMIA作为一个模型无关框架，成功实现了对白盒、灰盒和黑盒MIA和DI的增强，为隐私审计和版权执法提供了可扩展且有效的解决方案，验证了迭代轨迹信号优于传统单次生成方法。

Abstract: The tendency of large generative models to memorize training data makes sample verification critical for privacy auditing and copyright enforcement. Current membership (MIA) and dataset inference (DI) attacks often rely on one-shot generations, which yield weak signals and limited sensitivity across modalities. Inspired by Model Autophagy Disorder (MAD), we introduce MADreMIA, a model-agnostic framework that enhances white-, gray-, and black-box MIA and DI. Rather than relying on shadow model training -- often infeasible for large generative models -- our framework facilitates scalable inference by leveraging inherent signals through iterative trajectories. This process utilizes chained generations across diverse modalities, where each output serves as the subsequent input, to improve membership evidence at low FPR. We demonstrate that memorized training samples exhibit significantly higher coherence and slower degradation during iterative regeneration than non-member generations. Our results show that MADreMIA provides richer signals across diverse model families and modalities; we present comprehensive evaluations for IARs, diffusion, and language models, alongside preliminary results demonstrating its potential for audio models.

</details>


### [168] [Radial Suppression Accelerates Algorithmic Generalization: A Geometric Analysis of Delayed Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32000)
*Srijan Tiwari,Aditya Chauhan,Manjot Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 神经网络在泛化前会记忆算法训练数据。本文发现这种延迟由交叉熵优化下隐藏表示的径向膨胀驱动。提出抑制径向膨胀的惩罚项，使MLP和Transformer上的grokking加速6倍，并将10M参数nanoGPT的训练步数减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在能够泛化之前会经历长时间的记忆阶段（grokking现象），但这种现象背后的机制尚不清楚。本文旨在解释为什么神经网络会先记忆算法训练数据，然后才泛化，特别是针对需要发现结构化低维电路的任务。

Method: 作者提出激活空间动力学的径向-角度分解框架，推导出三个可测试命题。基于此，设计了一个单超参数范数惩罚项，软约束激活值在sqrt(d)半径的超球面上，从而抑制径向膨胀并强制主要进行角度更新。

Result: 理论方面：(i)惩罚径向膨胀产生各向异性、数据依赖的权重正则化；(ii)将径向梯度能量抑制到各向同性随机基线以下；(iii)使收敛偏向更平坦的极小值。实验方面：在模运算任务上，该惩罚使MLP和Transformer的grokking加速高达6倍；在3位数加法任务上，将10M参数nanoGPT的训练步数减少一半。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了记忆-泛化延迟的几何根源：径向膨胀。通过简单的激活范数惩罚抑制径向膨胀，可以显著加速算法任务上的泛化，为理解神经网络泛化机制提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Why do neural networks memorize algorithmic training data long before they generalize? We present a geometric case study demonstrating that, on tasks where generalization requires discovering structured low-dimensional circuits, the memorization-generalization delay is driven by radial inflation of hidden representations under cross-entropy optimization. We formalize a radial-angular decomposition of activation-space dynamics and derive three testable propositions: (i) that penalizing radial inflation induces anisotropic, data-dependent weight regularization; (ii) that it suppresses radial gradient energy below the isotropic random baseline, forcing predominantly angular updates; and (iii) that it biases convergence toward flatter minima. To empirically validate these propositions, we study a single-hyperparameter norm penalty that softly constrains activations to a sqrt(d)-radius hypersphere. On modular arithmetic, this penalty accelerates grokking up to 6x across MLPs and Transformers, and halves training steps for a 10M-parameter nanoGPT on 3-digit addition.

</details>


### [169] [Surrogate Fidelity: When Can Open LLMs Explain Closed Ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32008)
*Philippe Chlenski,Zachariah Carmichael,Ayush Warikoo,Chia-Tse Shao,Yingxiao Ye,Aobo Yang,Vivek Miglani,Nehal Bandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，即使不同模型在预测结果上一致，其内部归因机制往往不同；白盒信号（如注意力模式）稳定但因果解释力弱，黑盒方法更适合迁移研究。预测层面的相似性不足以支撑对闭源模型的机制性迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性（MI）研究需要完全访问模型内部参数，然而大多数主流部署的语言模型API仅提供输出token的对数概率。这产生了一个代理问题：在开源模型上进行的测量何时能够支持对闭源模型的推断？这一研究空白对于评估MI方法在实际闭源系统中的适用性至关重要。

Method: 研究者在预测、归因和表征三个层级评估代理保真度。针对二元分类任务，使用对数几率作为API兼容的模型表征空间标量读出，并采用留一法归因分析模型行为。研究覆盖了四个模型家族（Llama、Qwen、GPT和Gemini）共11个模型，系统比较白盒信号（注意力模式、扰动幅度）与黑盒方法（输入消融）的表现。

Result: 研究发现预测保真度显著高估了归因保真度：模型在答案上达成一致时，往往在原因上存在分歧。研究记录了访问-有效性倒置现象：白盒信号（注意力模式、扰动幅度）在模型间高度稳定，但对因果归因的预测能力微弱；而黑盒输入消融方法则天然能够捕捉因果归因。机制性洞察并不自动迁移到闭源目标模型。

Conclusion: 预测层面的模型一致性不足以保证机制性解释的迁移。研究者在应用MI方法于闭源模型时需谨慎，不能仅凭预测相似性就假设内部机制的可迁移性。代码和结果已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability (MI) requires full access to model internals, yet the APIs for most widely deployed language models at best expose log-probabilities over output tokens. This creates a surrogate problem: when do measurements made on open models allow us to make claims about a closed model? We evaluate surrogate fidelity at the prediction, attribution, and representation levels. For binary classification tasks, log-odds provide an API-compatible scalar readout of the model's representation space, and leave-one-out attributions provide insight into model behavior. Across eleven models spanning four families (Llama, Qwen, GPT, and Gemini), we find that prediction fidelity substantially overstates attribution fidelity: models that agree on what the answer is often disagree on why. We document an access-validity inversion: white-box signals like attention patterns and perturbation magnitudes are highly stable across models but only weakly predictive of causal attributions, which black-box input ablations capture by design. Mechanistic insight does not automatically transfer to closed targets, and prediction-level agreement is insufficient to warrant such transfer. Code and results are available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/surrogate.

</details>


### [170] [CoMet: Context and Multiplicity Decomposition for Multimodal Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32012)
*Sanghyuk Chun,William Yang,Amaya Dharmasiri,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoMet，一种针对多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的不确定性估计方法，通过将不确定性分解为上下文特定项和多重度特定项，并训练轻量级事后不确定性模块，在保持效率的同时显著提升了不确定性估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性估计是AI模型的长期挑战，即"知道自己不知道什么"，对于人类来说也是困难的（如达克效应）。虽然在简单分类系统中尚未完全解决，但在多模态大语言模型中解决这一问题变得日益重要。MLLM中的不确定性可能源于多种来源及其相互关系，也可能源于开放环境下的无界答案，现有方法难以有效处理这些复杂情况。

Method: 提出CoMet方法，将不确定性分解为两个组成部分：(1)上下文特定项，捕获给定上下文（如任务或提示）引起的歧义性；(2)多重度特定项，捕获由上下文确定的、与给定输入兼容的可信答案数量。训练一个轻量级事后不确定性模块来估计这些量，无需自回归答案生成或重复采样即可实现高效的不确定性估计。

Result: 在多个开放性多模态基准测试、幻觉检测任务和多项选择视觉问答基准上的实验表明，CoMet在不确定性估计方面持续优于现有基线方法，同时在实践中保持高效性。

Conclusion: CoMet通过创新的不确定性分解策略和轻量级模块设计，为多模态大语言模型的不确定性估计提供了有效且实用的解决方案，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。该方法为理解和量化MLLM的预测置信度提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation has been a long-standing challenge in AI models; it amounts to "knowing what you don't know," and metacognition is notoriously difficult even for humans (cf. the Dunning-Kruger effect). Although it is still far from solved even in simpler classification systems, tackling it in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is becoming increasingly important. Within MLLMs, uncertainty can stem from any of the diverse sources as well as from their relationships, and further can stem from the unbounded answers in the open-ended setting. To tackle the issues, we propose CoMet, an MLLM uncertainty estimation method by decomposing uncertainty into a context-specific term and a multiplicity-specific term. The former captures ambiguity induced by the given context (e.g., task or prompt), while the latter captures how many plausible answers determined by the context remain compatible with the given input. We train a lightweight post-hoc uncertainty module to estimate these quantities, which enables efficient uncertainty estimation without autoregressive answer generation or repeated sampling. Experiments on various open-ended multimodal benchmarks, hallucination detection, and multiple-choice visual question answering benchmarks show that CoMet consistently improves uncertainty estimation over existing baselines while remaining efficient in practice. Code is available at https://github.com/princetonvisualai/comet_uncertainty

</details>


### [171] [FedLAB: Traceable Semantic Codebooks for Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32016)
*Zekai Chen,Kairui Yang,Xuaner Chen,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedLAB框架，通过类型化层次化码本实现联邦多模态图基础学习，在保护隐私的同时提供语义可追溯性，性能提升最高7.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图基础模型需要从包含文本、图像、属性和关系拓扑的图中学习可重用知识。然而实际中多模态图分布在去中心化客户端，由于隐私限制无法集中共享原始内容和局部结构。现有方法通过参数、原型、嵌入或紧凑码本交换知识，但不明确展示模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文如何联合支持预测，缺乏语义可追溯性。

Method: 提出FedLAB可追溯语义码本框架，将多模态图知识组织为模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文的类型化层次化码本。通过联邦语义重心预训练优化这些可追溯单元，同时保持原始多模态内容和图结构在本地。

Result: 在10个基准数据集和6个下游任务上的广泛实验表明，FedLAB相比最先进的基线方法性能提升最高7.53%，同时保留了原生语义追溯接口。

Conclusion: FedLAB成功实现了联邦多模态图基础学习的两个关键目标：可迁移表示学习和严格数据隔离下的内在语义可追溯性，为隐私保护的多模态图学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal graph foundation models aim to learn reusable knowledge from graphs enriched with text, images, attributes, and relational topology, thereby supporting diverse graph-centric and modality-centric tasks. In practice, however, such multimodal graphs are often distributed across decentralized clients, where raw contents and local structures cannot be centrally shared due to privacy constraints. This motivates federated multimodal graph foundation learning, which requires not only transferable representation learning but also intrinsic semantic traceability under strict data isolation. Existing methods usually exchange or store knowledge through parameters, prototypes, embeddings, or compact codebooks, which support optimization and transfer but do not explicitly expose how modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context jointly support predictions. To bridge this gap, we propose FedLAB, a traceable semantic codebook framework that organizes multimodal graph knowledge into typed hierarchical codebooks for modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context. FedLAB further refines these trace units through federated semantic barycenter pre-training while keeping raw multimodal contents and graph structures local. Extensive experiments on 10 benchmarks and 6 downstream tasks show that FedLAB improves over state-of-the-art baselines by up to 7.53\%, while preserving a native semantic trace interface.

</details>


### [172] [TRIAGE: Role-Typed Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32017)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Jiaxin Zhang,Xinchen Du,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAGE通过引入角色类型的信用分配框架，解决了GRPO中结果信号的结构性不完整问题，在多个基准测试中显著提升了成功率并减少了环境交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO使用最终验证器结果作为对所有动作token的统一优势值，这种结果信号存在结构性缺陷：会惩罚失败轨迹中的有用探索，并在成功轨迹中强化冗余或倒退动作。这导致信用分配不准确，阻碍了智能体强化学习的有效训练。

Method: 提出TRIAGE框架，在结果信用基础上添加语义角色轴。结构化判断器将每个片段分类为决定性进展、有用探索、无进展基础设施或倒退四种角色类型，然后通过固定的角色条件规则将这些标签映射为有界的片段级过程奖励。该方法证明角色条件信用是从角色标签单独可表达的最优片段级修正。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop三个基准测试中，TRIAGE相比GRPO提高了两个策略模型的成功率，并优于标量判断器派生的过程奖励和结果监督的共享主干价值基线。消融实验显示主要收益来自角色类型化而非仅仅添加密集奖励。在ALFWorld和WebShop的完成轨迹上，TRIAGE相比GRPO额外减少了10.4%和14.8%的环境交互轮次。

Conclusion: 可靠地检测成功轨迹内部的倒退行为是主要贡献因素，探索信用提供一致的次要收益。TRIAGE成功解决了结果信用分配的两个主要盲点，证明了角色类型化信用分配在智能体强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning requires assigning credit to environment-facing actions such as searches, clicks, edits, navigation commands, and object interactions. Standard GRPO uses the final verifier outcome as a uniform advantage over all action tokens. This outcome signal is useful but structurally incomplete: it punishes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts. We propose TRIAGE, a role-typed credit assignment framework that adds a semantic role axis to outcome credit. A structured judge classifies each segment as decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression, and a fixed role-conditioned rule maps these labels to bounded segment-level process rewards. This keeps verifier outcomes as the source of optimization direction while correcting the two main blind spots of outcome-only credit. We further show that role-conditioned credit is the optimal segment-level correction expressible from role labels alone -- a projection of the per-segment advantage residual onto the role variable -- so that the fixed role constants reduce advantage estimation error whenever the judge is reliable, and we connect this to lower-variance policy gradients. Across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop, TRIAGE improves success rates over GRPO for two policy models and outperforms both a scalar judge-derived process reward and an outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline. Ablations show that the gain comes from role typing rather than merely adding dense rewards: reliable detection of regression inside successful trajectories is the dominant contributor, while exploration credit provides a consistent secondary gain; on completed ALFWorld and WebShop rollouts, TRIAGE also reduces environment-facing turns by an additional $10.4\%$ and $14.8\%$ relative to GRPO.

</details>


### [173] [SemRF: A Semantic Reference Frame for Residual-Stream Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32022)
*Jian Gu,Aldeida Aleti,Chunyang Chen,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义参考框架(SemRF)，解决语言模型残差流分析中嵌入与反嵌入坐标不一致的问题，将语义测量与残差动态分离，提供稳定坐标和理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的残差流分析通过中间层解码研究语言模型计算过程，但不同层的嵌入锚点与反嵌入读取坐标可能不一致，导致观察到的变化可能反映测量漂移而非真实计算过程，缺乏统一的语义坐标系。

Method: 引入语义参考框架(SemRF)形式化方法：1) 固定锚点并将状态相对于锚点测量；2) 使用伪逆绑定实现精确同步；3) 建立语义Voronoi图，通过距离或证据（如logits）将各层分配到粗粒度单元；4) 定义层间步长、贡献分布和不平衡诊断；5) 构建边界松弛管道，定义其中的最小作用规范轨迹。

Result: 在受限双可逆条件下获得：1) 稳定的语义基坐标；2) 失真界限和近似恒等变化；3) 规范轨迹的唯一性（满足正二次权重时）；4) 离散样条方程（远离活跃约束时）；5) 低曲率意味着分段线性可压缩性和局部知识密度；6) 低复杂度轨迹使用更少语义自由度，建立与参数效率的条件性联系。

Conclusion: SemRF框架成功将语义测量与残差动态分离，为语言模型深度方向的计算分析提供了理论基础和实用工具。通过参数到轨迹的映射，证明了较低作用和较低复杂度的轨迹在拟合数据的可接受设置中使用更少的语义自由度，建立了与参数效率的条件联系，但保证需要控制接口误差和显式管道约束下的小投影残差。

Abstract: Residual-stream analysis asks how language-model computation evolves across depth, but intermediate decoding requires comparable readout coordinates across layers. If embedding anchors and unembedding readout disagree on the chosen span, apparent motion may reflect measurement drift rather than computation. We introduce \emph{Semantic Reference Frames} (SemRF), an anchor-based formalism separating semantic measurement from residual dynamics. A SemRF fixes anchors and measures states against them. Pseudo-inverse tying gives exact synchronization; under restricted bi-invertibility, SemRF yields stable semantic-basis coordinates, distortion bounds, and near-identity changes. With the frame fixed, residual computation becomes a depthwise semantic trajectory. The anchors induce a semantic Voronoi diagram: distance, or evidence such as logits, assigns each layer to a coarse cell, while coordinates retain within-cell motion and margins. We define layerwise steps, contribution profiles, and imbalance diagnostics, then use the Voronoi trace to define a margin-relaxed tube. The canonical trace is the minimum-action path inside this tube; when nonempty with positive quadratic weight, it is unique and obeys a discrete spline equation away from active constraints. Excess action controls step, curvature, and profile mismatch. Low curvature implies piecewise-linear compressibility and local knowledge density: lower trace complexity means fewer semantic knots. Through the parameter-to-trajectory map, this gives a conditional link to parameter efficiency: among admissible settings fitting data, lower-action and lower-complexity traces use fewer semantic degrees of freedom. The guarantees require controlled interface error and small projection residual under explicit tube constraints.

</details>


### [174] [AdaJEPA: An Adaptive Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32026)
*Ying Wang,Oumayma Bounou,Yann LeCun,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaJEPA，一种自适应潜在世界模型，通过在MPC闭环中进行测试时自适应，解决了传统冻结模型在分布偏移下规划失败的问题，仅需少量梯度更新即可显著提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在世界模型在测试时通常保持冻结状态，当面对测试时的分布偏移导致预测不准确时，规划容易失败。

Method: 提出AdaJEPA自适应潜在世界模型，在模型预测控制（MPC）闭环中执行测试时自适应：规划并执行第一个动作块后，利用观测到的下一状态转换作为自监督自适应信号更新模型，然后使用更新后的模型重新规划。

Result: 在一系列目标达成任务中，AdaJEPA在每个MPC重规划步骤仅需一次梯度更新的情况下，显著提高了规划成功率，且无需额外的专家演示。

Conclusion: 通过在MPC闭环中持续进行测试时自适应，AdaJEPA能够有效应对分布偏移，实现了世界模型的持续校准，为高维观测下的规划任务提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from high-dimensional observations by predicting future states in a compact latent space. However, these models are typically kept frozen at test time: when their predictions become inaccurate, planning can fail, especially under test-time distribution shift. To address this, we propose AdaJEPA, an adaptive latent world model that performs test-time adaptation within the closed loop of model predictive control (MPC). After training, AdaJEPA plans and executes the first action chunk, uses the observed next-state transition as a self-supervised adaptation signal, and replans with the updated model. This closed-loop update continuously recalibrates the world model without additional expert demonstrations. Across a range of goal-reaching tasks, AdaJEPA substantially improves planning success with as few as one gradient step per MPC replanning step.

</details>


### [175] [QVal: Cheaply Evaluating Dense Supervision Signals for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32034)
*Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez,Matteo Merler,Ilze Amanda Auzina,Joschka Strüber,Ameya Prabhu,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了QVal，一个免训练的测试平台，用于直接评估密集监督信号的质量，发现简单的提示基线方法在评估中持续优于现有的复杂密集监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长周期LLM代理任务中，仅依赖结果奖励过于稀疏，无法有效指导中间动作。现有密集监督方法通过评分中间步骤来缓解此问题，但评估这些方法通常依赖于集成它们的下游训练性能，这种方式成本高昂、混淆了监督质量与训练工程因素，且不同方法家族因训练设置不同而难以公平比较。

Method: 引入QVal测试平台，给定状态-动作对，评估方法的得分是否具有Q对齐性（Q-alignment），即是否根据强参考策略的Q值对动作进行排序。该方法无需训练即可直接比较不同密集监督信号的信号质量，将其与其他工程选择分离。实现了QVal-v1.0，涵盖四个环境、七个方法家族、21种密集监督方法和六个开源模型骨干，进行了超过1.2K次评估实验。

Result: 实验发现简单的提示基线方法在所有评估中持续优于文献中的复杂密集监督方法；性能表现强烈聚类于方法家族；这些发现跨模型规模、环境和观测模态均保持一致。

Conclusion: QVal提供了一个可扩展的框架，使研究人员能够在训练前迭代优化密集监督方法，实现了对密集监督信号的独立、公平评估，揭示了当前复杂方法相比简单基线存在不足，为未来方法开发提供了基准。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act over long horizons, where a single trajectory can contain hundreds or thousands of actions. In these settings, outcome-only rewards provide too sparse guidance, failing to inform the model about the goodness of intermediate actions. Dense supervision methods aim to solve this problem by scoring intermediate steps, from intrinsic confidence to self-distillation and embedding similarities. However, it is common practice to evaluate them by measuring the downstream performance of a training pipeline that integrates them. This is expensive, conflates supervision quality with training engineering confounders, and renders different methodological families requiring distinct training setups incomparable. As a result, dense supervision methods are rarely benchmarked on common ground. We introduce QVal, a training-free testbed for directly evaluating dense supervision signals. Given a state-action pair, QVal measures how well a method's score is Q-aligned: whether it orders actions according to the Q-values of a strong reference-policy. This lets us compare signals before any training run and separate signal quality from other engineering choices. We instantiate QVal as QVal-v1.0, benchmarking 21 dense supervision methods across four diverse environments and seven methodological families, with over 1.2K evaluation experiments across six open-weight model backbones. We find that simple prompting baselines consistently outperform recent dense supervision methods from the literature, and that performance clusters strongly by family. These findings hold across model sizes, environments, and observation modalities. QVal is designed to be easily extensible to new environments and methods, enabling researchers to iterate on dense supervision methods before any training run.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [CALO: Constraint-Aware Learning Optimization for Joint Resource Allocation in Double-Active RIS-Assisted Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30803)
*Alaa S. Arabiyat,Mohammad J. Abdel-Rahman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了约束感知学习优化(CALO)框架，用于解决双主动RIS辅助无线系统中的联合资源分配问题，实现了100%可行性、优于传统BCD算法的可达速率以及显著降低的在线推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双主动可重构智能表面(RIS)辅助无线系统能够改善阻塞主导环境下的覆盖和可达速率，但其联合资源分配由于RIS放置、放大功率分配和反射元素分配之间的耦合关系而具有挑战性。该问题具有线性约束、非凸特性，且涉及连续和离散变量，使得传统迭代求解器（如块坐标下降法BCD）在实时部署中计算代价过高。

Method: 提出约束感知学习优化(CALO)框架，将决策变量重新表述为分组分数表示，通过保约束变换映射到物理资源，确保距离、功率和元素预算约束在构造上得到满足。引入直通估计器实现离散反射元素分配的可微学习，同时使用基于遗憾的铰链目标，以BCD解为参考并鼓励超越求解器模仿的性能改进。

Result: CALO在所有测试配置中实现100%可行性，在城市和农村场景下均提高了相对BCD的可达速率，并将在线推理时间降低了数个数量级。仿真结果验证了结构感知学习在主动多RIS无线系统中的可行性和实时优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构感知学习方法能够有效解决主动多RIS无线系统中的可行性和实时优化问题，CALO框架为复杂约束下的联合资源分配提供了一种高效的数据驱动解决方案。

Abstract: Double-active reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted wireless systems can improve coverage and achievable rate in blockage-dominated environments. Still, their joint resource allocation is challenging due to the coupling among RIS placement, amplification power allocation, and reflecting-element assignment. The resulting problem is linearly constrained, non-convex, and involves both continuous and discrete variables, making conventional iterative solvers such as block coordinate descent (BCD) computationally expensive for real-time deployment. This paper proposes a \underline{c}onstraint-\underline{a}ware \underline{l}earning \underline{o}ptimization (CALO) framework for data-driven joint resource allocation in double-active RIS-assisted networks. CALO reformulates the decision variables into grouped fractional representations and maps them to physical resources through constraint-preserving transformations, ensuring that distance, power, and element-budget constraints are satisfied by construction. A straight-through estimator is incorporated to enable differentiable learning over discrete reflecting-element assignments, while a regret-driven hinge objective uses the BCD solution as a reference and encourages performance improvement beyond solver imitation. Simulation results show that CALO achieves $100\%$ feasibility across all tested configurations, improves the achievable rate over BCD in both urban and rural scenarios, and reduces online inference time by orders of magnitude. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of structure-aware learning for feasible and real-time optimization in active multi-RIS wireless systems.

</details>


### [177] [A Practical Implementation of Day-3 Cooperative Intersection with Automated Connected Mini-Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30838)
*Lorenzo Farina,Vittorio Todisco,Federico Gavioli,Salvatore Iandolo,Francesco Moretti,Giuseppe Perrone,Matteo Piccoli,Francesco Raviglione,Marco Rapelli,Antonio Solida,Claudio Casetti,Paolo Burgio,Carlo Augusto Grazia,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文利用网联自主微型小车实现了协作式交叉口管理的实时演示验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交叉口控制机制（如交通信号灯）会引入延误和不必要的停车，而由网联自动驾驶车辆赋能的协作驾驶有望提升交通效率与安全性；然而现有研究多集中于算法，实验演示仍然有限。

Method: 搭建由多辆1:10比例自主驾驶小车构成的实验平台，车辆配备无线通信模块，与中央控制器交互；车辆接近交叉口时与控制器通信，由控制器统一调度并下发通行轨迹，车辆据此以不停车方式通过交叉口。

Result: 成功实现了协作式交叉口管理的实时演示，车辆在中央调度下无停驶通过交叉口，验证了自主驾驶、无线通信与协作控制在统一平台上的集成与可行性。

Conclusion: 该平台为未来智能交通系统的协作式交叉口管理概念提供了实用、可复现的实验验证环境，具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Cooperative driving enabled by connected and automated vehicles is expected to improve traffic efficiency and safety, particularly at intersections where traditional control mechanisms such as traffic lights introduce delays and unnecessary stops. Although cooperative intersection management algorithms have been widely studied, experimental demonstrations remain limited. This paper presents a real-time demonstration of cooperative intersection management using connected autonomous mini-cars. The testbed consists of multiple 1:10 scale vehicles equipped with autonomous driving capabilities and wireless communication modules that interact with a centralized controller responsible for scheduling their crossing of the intersection. Vehicles approaching the intersection exchange messages with the controller to set the appropriate mobility profile to traverse the intersection without stopping. The demonstration integrates autonomous driving, wireless communication, and cooperative control in a single experimental platform, providing a practical environment for validating cooperative intersection management concepts for future intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [178] [Budget-Adaptive Routing: Skipping the Weak When the Strong Answers Anyway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30919)
*Wei Geng,Nitinder Mohan,Jörg Ott*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预算自适应路由机制，通过在弱模型前部署轻量级路由估计器（比弱检测器轻29倍），并根据卸载预算动态选择路由策略，在PASCAL VOC上将延迟降低30%，某些情况下准确率甚至超过强模型1.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边缘-云推理协作系统将路由估计器置于弱检测器之后，导致即使后续被卸载到云端，边缘端仍需执行弱模型前向传播，造成计算资源浪费。当卸载预算变化时，这种弱条件设计可能并非最优。

Method: 提出两种方案：(1)弱跳过估计器——直接从原始像素提取路由信号，避免执行弱模型前向传播；(2)预算自适应路由——通过两个离线调节的阈值，根据卸载预算在弱跳过和弱条件部署之间动态选择，追踪两种策略的最优边界。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC数据集上，预算自适应路由器追踪了两种固定部署策略的精度上包络线；单帧延迟降低高达19.1ms（约30%）；在部分操作点上以更少计算量超越强模型峰值mAP达1.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 弱跳过和弱条件部署各有优势区域，预算自适应路由能够根据卸载预算自动选择最优策略，在准确率-延迟权衡中达到最佳性能，优于现有SOTA方法。

Abstract: Edge-cloud inference collaborations are often designed with a routing estimator that decides whether to offload each frame from weak models at the edge to stronger models in the cloud. Existing systems place the routing estimator after the weak detector, so the weak forward pass still runs even on frames that are later offloaded. In this paper, we argue that this weak-conditioned design can be suboptimal when the offload budget varies. First, we present a competitive weak-skipping estimator (0.153 GFLOPs, about 29x lighter than the weak detector at 4.49 GFLOPs) that extracts routing signal from raw pixels, outperforming the common after-weak placement weak-conditioned baselines. Second, we show that neither weak-skipping nor weak-conditioned placement dominates across the full operating curve, and we propose budget-adaptive routing, which selects between them by offload budget via two offline-tuned thresholds. On PASCAL VOC, our budget-adaptive router traces the upper accuracy envelope of both fixed placements across the operating range. Our method reduces per-frame latency by up to 19.1 ms (about 30% lower at rho = 0.9). Besides outperforming SOTA methods, it is surprisingly stronger than the strong model (+1.7 pp over the strong model's peak mAP) at some operating points with far less compute. Artifacts are available at https://github.com/ViGeng/bgt-ada

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Wireless Security: Covert Communications in Large Language Model-enabled Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31016)
*Yuanai Xie,Jiaxin Chen,Zhaozhi Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型边缘网络(LLMEN)的安全挑战，提出了一种隐蔽通信与计算方法，在保障安全隐私的同时提升执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMEN在6G时代面临独特的安全威胁，包括无线信道窃听、干扰、提示词中毒和注入攻击等。由于LLM的固有复杂性、高计算开销以及与用户的持续交互特性，现有防御措施往往带来难以接受的开销，亟需开发整体性、高效且安全的隐私保护方案。

Method: 提出隐蔽通信与计算(CC)方法来增强LLMEN的整体安全性和效率；开发多种补充方案来提升框架的隐蔽性；通过案例研究对方法进行评估，在严格的通信和计算安全要求下最小化总延迟。

Result: 数值实验结果表明，所提出的方法在增强隐私保护和大语言模型任务执行效率方面具有显著有效性，能够在安全约束下优化系统性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的隐蔽通信与计算框架能够有效应对LLMENs的安全威胁，为6G时代的边缘智能服务提供了可行的安全解决方案，实现了隐私保护与执行效率的双重优化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-enabled edge networks (LLMENs) offer mobile users high-quality and low-latency AI-generated content services in the 6G era. However, unlike typical edge networks, LLMENs present unique security challenges due to the inherent complexity of LLMs, their high computational overhead, and continuous interactions with users. Specifically, both frequent user interactions (i.e., queries and responses) over wireless channels and potential electromagnetic information leakage from intensive LLM computations make LLMENs susceptible to various security threats, such as eavesdropping, jamming, prompt poisoning, and prompt injection attacks. Since existing countermeasures against these attacks often incur prohibitive overhead, developing holistic, efficient, and secure privacy protections for LLMENs is crucial. This article first reviews the vulnerabilities of LLMENs, outlines various attacks, and analyzes the drawbacks of existing countermeasures. To overcome these limitations, we propose a covert communications (CC) and computations approach to enhance both the overall security and efficiency of LLMENs. Furthermore, various supplementary solutions are developed to improve the covertness of this framework. Finally, our approach is further evaluated through a case study where the total latency is minimized under stringent communication and computational security requirements. Numerical results demonstrate the proposed approach's effectiveness in enhancing both privacy protection and the execution efficiency of LLM tasks.

</details>


### [180] [Latency-Sensitive 5G RAN Slicing for Deterministic Aperiodic Traffic in Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31499)
*M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Jan García-Morales,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 现有RAN切片方案仅考虑传输速率，无法满足工业确定性非周期流量的严格时延要求。本文提出联合考虑传输速率和时延需求的RAN切片设计方法，可有效支持关键服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G及未来网络通过网络切片支持智能制造中的关键业务，但现有RAN切片方案仅基于传输速率需求进行资源划分，无法保障工业关键应用中确定性非周期流量的严格时延要求，因此需要新的切片设计方法。

Method: 提出使用同时考虑服务传输速率需求和时延需求的描述符来设计RAN切片，而非仅基于传输速率进行资源划分。

Result: 证明了仅考虑传输速率的现有方法不适合保障确定性非周期流量的严格时延要求；验证了所提出的联合考虑传输速率和时延的切片设计方法能够有效支持产生确定性非周期流量的关键服务。

Conclusion: 为了在5G及未来网络中支持具有严格时延要求的工业关键应用，RAN切片设计必须同时考虑服务的传输速率和时延需求，这对于支持确定性非周期流量至关重要。

Abstract: 5G and beyond networks will support the digitalization of smart manufacturing thanks to their capacity to simultaneously serve different types of traffic with distinct QoS requirements. This can be achieved using Network Slicing that creates different logical network partitions (or slices) over a common infrastructure, and each can be tailored to support a particular type of traffic. The configuration of the Radio Access Network (RAN) slices strongly impacts the capacity of 5G and beyond to support critical services with stringent QoS requirements, and in particular deterministic requirements. Existing RAN Slicing solutions only consider the transmission rate (or bandwidth) requirements of the different services to partition the radio resources. This study demonstrates that this approach is not suitable to guarantee the stringent latency requirements of deterministic aperiodic traffic that is characteristic of industrial critical applications. We then propose designing RAN slices using descriptors that consider both the services' transmission rate and latency requirements, and demonstrate that this approach can support critical services that generate deterministic aperiodic traffic.

</details>


### [181] [Configured Grant Scheduling for the Support of TSN Traffic in 5G and Beyond Industrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31506)
*M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Ana Larrañaga,Javier Gozalvez,Imanol Martínez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种5G配置授权调度方案，通过协调5G与TSN调度器，利用TSN流量特征信息，有效支持多周期TSN流量，性能优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络原生不支持确定性通信，而工业网络日益依赖TSN来支持有界时延的确定性通信，因此需要将5G与TSN集成以支持灵活可重构的工厂。

Method: 提出一种新颖的5G配置授权调度方案，协调5G和TSN调度器；该方案利用TSN流量的特征信息（数据包大小、周期性和到达时间）来指导调度决策。

Result: 所提方案在支持具有不同周期的多个TSN流量流的能力方面，优于目前最先进的技术。

Conclusion: 通过协调5G与TSN调度器并利用流量特征信息，可以有效解决5G不支持确定性通信的局限，提升对工业TSN流量的支持能力。

Abstract: 5G and beyond networks can facilitate the digital transformation of manufacturing and support more flexible and reconfigurable factories with ubiquitous mobile connectivity. This requires integrating 5G networks with industrial networks that increasingly rely on TSN (Time Sensitive Networking) to support deterministic communications with bounded latencies. Deterministic communications are critical for many industrial applications, but 5G does not natively support deterministic communications. To address this limitation, this study proposes the coordination of the 5G and TSN schedulers and presents a novel 5G configured grant scheduling scheme to support TSN traffic. The scheme uses information about the characteristics of the TSN traffic (packet size, periodicity, and arrival time) to coordinate its scheduling decisions with the TSN scheduler. The study demonstrates that the proposed scheme outperforms the state-of-the-art in the capacity to support multiple TSN traffic flows with different periodicities.

</details>


### [182] [Support of Teleoperated Driving with 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31512)
*M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Baldomero Coll-Perales,Mohammad Irfan Khan,Sergei S. Avedisov,Onur Altintas,Javier Gozalvez,Miguel Sepulcre*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了利用5G网络支持远程驾驶(ToD)的可行性，发现其关键取决于带宽和TDD帧结构，且在扩展车辆数量时需要降低视频码率，而传统集中式5G部署可能难以满足最严格的延迟要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶(ToD)可以支持自动驾驶车辆应对复杂或意外的交通场景，但ToD需要可靠且低延迟的通信连接，因此需要研究5G网络是否能够满足这些严格的通信需求。

Method: 通过分析5G网络的带宽能力和时分双工(TDD)帧结构对上下行传输带宽分配的影响，评估5G网络支持ToD的可行性，并研究多车辆场景下的扩展性和传统集中式5G网络部署的延迟性能。

Result: 研究发现：1) ToD的可行性很大程度上取决于带宽和TDD帧结构；2) 扩展5G支持的ToD车辆数量需要降低视频比特率；3) 传统集中式5G网络部署由于互联网连接引入的延迟，可能无法满足最严格的ToD延迟要求。

Conclusion: 5G网络支持ToD是可行的，但存在明显限制条件，需要根据带宽、TDD配置和部署架构来优化网络设计，可能需要分布式部署或边缘计算方案来满足严格的延迟需求。

Abstract: Teleoperated driving (ToD) can support autonomous driving under complex or unexpected traffic scenarios that an autonomous vehicle may not understand or be able to handle. In ToD, autonomous vehicles transmit video feeds and perception data to the remote control center. The operator uses this data to understand the driving environment and remotely control the vehicle that can take over the control once the scenario is resolved. ToD requires reliable and low latency communications between the vehicle and the ToD control center. This study analyzes the feasibility to support ToD with 5G networks. The study demonstrates that the feasibility strongly depends on the bandwidth and the Time Division Duplexing (TDD) frame structure that conditions how the bandwidth is distributed between uplink and downlink transmissions. The study also shows that scaling the number of 5G-supported ToD vehicles requires the vehicles to reduce the video bitrates. The study also shows that traditional centralized 5G network deployments may be challenged by some of the most stringent ToD latency requirements due to the latency introduced by the Internet connection to the ToD control center.

</details>


### [183] [Semantic-Aware Multiple Access via Spatial Redundancy Exploitation for Uplink-Dominant 6G Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31715)
*Hamidreza Mazandarani,Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb,Onur Günlü*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义感知的多址接入方案，通过利用车载用户间重叠的视野来减少上行链路中的冗余传输，从而提高6G上行系统的资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的上行主导型6G应用（如协同车辆流媒体）需要在有限的无线资源上高效传输大量视觉数据。现有语义通信方法通常独立处理用户，忽略了邻近设备观测之间的空间冗余，导致资源浪费。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段方法：首先，邻近车辆通过车对车（V2V）链路共享选定的观测图像块以计算用户间的空间冗余；其次，用户仅向基站传输语义重要且非冗余的图像块，基站利用接收到的图像块和邻近车辆的互补视图进行观测重建。该方法制定了联合感知与传输控制问题，决策用户应传输哪些图像块、何时传输以及使用哪个信道。

Result: 在密集城市车辆场景中的仿真结果表明，该方法提高了实现高保真重建的用户比例，验证了语义感知多址接入在提升6G上行系统资源效率方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 语义感知多址接入技术通过利用车辆间的空间冗余，能够显著提升上行链路的资源利用效率，为实现可持续、资源高效的6G上行系统提供了重要潜力。

Abstract: Emerging uplink-dominant 6G use cases, such as cooperative vehicular streaming, require efficient transmission of high-volume visual data over limited wireless resources. While semantic communications can reduce traffic by prioritizing task-relevant content, most existing approaches treat users independently and therefore overlook spatial redundancy among nearby devices' observations. This paper proposes a semantic-aware multiple access scheme that exploits overlapping fields of view among vehicular users to reduce redundant uplink transmissions. We formulate a joint perception and transmission control problem in which users decide which image patches to transmit, when to transmit them, and over which channel, subject to communication constraints. To address the resulting complexity, we introduce a practical two-phase approach. First, nearby vehicles share selected observation patches over Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) links to calculate inter-user spatial redundancy. Second, users transmit only semantically important, non-redundant patches to the base station, where observations can be reconstructed using the received patches and complementary views from neighboring vehicles. Simulation results in a dense urban vehicular scenario demonstrate that our approach improves the proportion of users who achieve high-fidelity reconstruction, highlighting the potential of semantic-aware multiple access for sustainable and resource-efficient 6G uplink systems.

</details>


### [184] [LEO Satellite Network Orchestration with Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31950)
*Aruna Jayarajan,N. Cameron Matson,Karthikeyan Sundaresan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NEO-GNN，一种基于异构图神经网络的学习型网络编排框架，用于解决低地球轨道（LEO）卫星星座中动态卫星与地面节点之间的协调挑战，实现了全覆盖和负载均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座对于扩展全球互联网接入至关重要，但其高度动态性（由网络移动性引起）导致卫星与地面节点（网关和地面设备）之间的协调复杂。有限的卫星可见窗口和空间上不平衡的用户流量需求使得本地关联策略（如最近卫星或贪婪负载选择）导致部分地面覆盖或负载不平衡，影响流量需求的满足。

Method: 提出了NEO-GNN框架，将卫星-地面网络建模为动态时空图。采用异构图神经网络（GNN），将卫星、网关和地面小区建模为不同的节点类型，以捕捉它们不同的可见性和网络能力。通过定制的损失函数以无监督方式进行训练，平衡覆盖和利用的双重要求，并在推理时产生高效、实时的关联决策。

Result: NEO-GNN实现了完整的地面小区覆盖，通过平衡的卫星和网关使用提高了流量需求满足率，并且在动态可见性和部分卫星故障情况下保持鲁棒性。评估表明该框架可作为传统优化方法的可扩展且高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: NEO-GNN为透镜式LEO卫星系统中的实时网络编排提供了一种可扩展且高效的替代方案，能够在保证全覆盖的同时实现负载均衡，优于传统的集中式优化方法。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are becoming essential for expanding global Internet access, especially in remote and under-served areas. However, their highly dynamic nature, arising from network mobility, introduces complex coordination challenges between the dynamic satellites and the ground nodes (gateways and terrestrial devices). This is underscored by limited satellite visibility windows and spatially imbalanced user traffic demands. Local association (cell-satellite-gateway) strategies, such as nearest-satellite or greedy load-based selection, result in partial terrestrial coverage or lead to load imbalance that affects traffic demand fulfillment. Network-driven orchestration through centralized optimization can strike an efficient balance between these two key objectives, but is often computationally intensive for periodic operation and real-time deployment. This work presents a learning-based network orchestration framework, NEO-GNN, that models a satellite-ground network as a dynamic spatiotemporal graph. In contrast to prior works, it employs a heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (GNN), where satellites, gateways, and ground cells are modeled as distinct node types to capture their varied visibility and networking capabilities. They are trained in an unsupervised manner using tailored loss functions to balance the dual requirements of coverage and utilization, and produce efficient, real-time association decisions during inference. Evaluations show that NEO-GNN delivers complete ground-cell coverage, improves traffic demand satisfaction through balanced satellite and gateway use, and remains robust under dynamic visibility and partial satellite failures. NEO-GNN provides a scalable and efficient alternative to traditional optimization methods for real-time network orchestration in bent-pipe LEO satellite systems.

</details>


### [185] [Semantic Leakage and Privacy Preservation in Relay-Assisted Semantic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31973)
*Yalin E. Sagduyu,Tugba Erpek,Aylin Yener,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了中继辅助语义通信系统存在隐私漏洞，并提出了一种迭代对抗训练框架来保护语义信息不被中继节点窃取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信作为一种新兴范式，通过优先传输任务相关信息来实现高效鲁棒的通信。然而，在中继辅助的语义通信系统中，中继节点直接处理学习到的潜在表示，这带来了严重的隐私风险——即使无法访问源数据，中继节点仍能可靠地推断语义含义并重建信号，性能可与合法接收者相当。这一发现揭示了语义表示的根本隐私脆弱性，亟需有效的保护机制。

Method: 提出了一种迭代对抗训练框架，显式考虑中继处经过自适应训练的强窃听者。该方法交替优化中继的窃听功能和合法系统，通过对抗训练生成能够在合法接收者处保持语义解码性能，同时降低中继处语义推断能力的表示。这种保护以隐蔽方式实现，在保持高重建保真度的同时选择性抑制语义泄漏。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著扩大了合法接收者与窃听者之间的语义准确率差距，且该差距在各种信道条件下均保持稳定。重要的是，这种隐私保护是在保持高重建保真度的同时实现的，实现了隐蔽的语义泄漏抑制。

Conclusion: 本研究首次揭示了中继辅助语义通信系统的根本隐私漏洞，并成功提出了有效的对抗训练解决方案。该框架能够在不影响合法通信性能的前提下，有效防御中继节点的语义窃听攻击，为语义通信系统的安全设计提供了新的思路和方法。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SemCom) has emerged as a promising paradigm in which the transmission of task-relevant information is prioritized over raw data, enabling efficient and robust communication under resource and channel constraints. In this paper, the privacy implications of relay-assisted SemCom systems are studied, where the intermediate relay node operates directly on learned latent representations. It is shown that the relay, even without access to source data, can reliably infer semantic meaning and reconstruct signals with performance comparable to that of the legitimate receiver, revealing a fundamental privacy vulnerability of semantic representations. To address this issue, an iterative adversarial training framework is proposed in which a strong, adaptively trained eavesdropper at the relay is explicitly accounted for. The proposed approach alternates between optimizing the relay's eavesdropping function and the legitimate system, resulting in representations that preserve semantic decoding performance at the intended receiver while degrading semantic inference at the relay. The semantic accuracy gap between the legitimate receiver and the eavesdropper is significantly enlarged across channel conditions. Importantly, this protection is achieved in a stealthy manner, with high reconstruction fidelity maintained while semantic leakage is selectively suppressed.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Protecting Futures against Silent Data Corruption -- Efficient Task Replication for Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30771)
*Rüdiger Nather,Claudia Fohry,Mia Reitz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在动态异步任务运行时中通过紧密耦合原始计算与副本计算来实现静默数据损坏(SDC)检测与修复的方法，以小于2倍的时间开销代价实现全复制保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算问题规模增大，静默数据损坏(SDC)发生概率增加。现有SDC保护方案主要针对静态任务和依赖关系，而动态设置(尤其是集群环境)更具挑战性，因为需要追踪任务/数据以进行比较。动态设置中的任务运行时生成、任务通信、条件触碰和集群负载均衡等特性使得传统的复制验证方法难以直接应用。

Method: 提出一种将原始计算与副本计算紧密耦合的方法：1) 在与运行时系统交互时对所有输出效应进行交叉验证；2) 选择性地重新计算受影响的任务；3) 针对具有运行时任务生成、C++11风格的promise/future任务通信、条件触碰以及work-first工作窃取的集群负载均衡等动态特性进行了专门设计；4) 在ItoyoriFBC运行时系统中实现了该方法。

Result: 在Fibonacci和模拟H矩阵LU分解基准测试上的初步实验表明：1) 尽管采用全复制，无故障运行时间的增长因子仍小于2，这主要得益于任务数量增加带来的负载均衡改进机会；2) 每次SDC的故障纠正开销约占总体运行时间的0.5%。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地在高度动态的异步任务环境中实现了高效的SDC检测与修复，通过紧密耦合的复制验证策略和选择性重计算，在保证可靠性的同时将性能开销控制在可接受范围内，证明了在动态设置中进行SDC保护的可行性。

Abstract: As the size of computational problems grows, so does the likelihood of Silent Data Corruptions (SDCs). A common defense is replication, where the computation is repeated and correct results are determined by majority voting. Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes are generally well suited for this approach, since the inputs and outputs of task replicas can be compared, and the tasks can be recomputed if necessary. Most existing SDC protection schemes assume static tasks and dependencies. Dynamic settings are more challenging, especially in clusters, since the tasks/data must be tracked for the comparisons.
  This paper considers a particularly dynamic setting with task spawning at runtime, task communication through C++11-like promises/futures, conditional touches, and cluster-wide load balancing via work-first work stealing. We propose an approach that closely couples original and replica computations by cross-validating all outgoing effects when interacting with the runtime system. The approach selectively recomputes affected tasks only.
  We implemented the approach in the ItoyoriFBC runtime system and conducted preliminary experiments with Fibonacci and emulated $\mathcal{H}$-matrix LU decomposition benchmarks. Results show a factor of less than two increase of failure-free running times, despite full replication, which is mainly due to improved opportunities for load balancing resulting from the higher number of tasks. The overhead for failure correction was about 0.5% of the overall running time per SDC.

</details>


### [187] [StreamGuard: Low-Overhead Resilience for Real-time HPC Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30848)
*Hai Duc Nguyen,Bogdan Nicolae,Tekin Bicer,Amal Gueroudji,Matthieu Dorier,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态异步非阻塞检查点机制和进度感知的负载重分配策略，显著提升了生产者-消费者流处理模式在故障环境下的弹性能力，将故障影响降低至多6倍，且无故障执行开销低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时科学工作流运行在连续数据流上，需要在复杂且易发生故障的基础设施上产生及时、高质量的结果。硬件故障、网络中断以及由资源争用或系统异构性导致的性能异常会严重降低性能并违反实时约束，因此需要增强生产者-消费者流处理模式的弹性。

Method: 提出了两种互补技术：(i) 动态异步非阻塞检查点机制，在不中断计算的情况下保留进度；(ii) 进度感知的负载重分配策略，检测慢速工作节点并主动重新平衡任务。这两种机制协同工作，在高错误率环境中维持前向进展和平衡执行。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法将故障和性能异常的影响降低至多6倍，同时在无故障执行中引入的开销低于1%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效增强了生产者-消费者流处理模式在易错环境中的弹性能力，能够在保持低开销的同时显著减少故障对系统性能的影响，为实时科学工作流提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time scientific workflows operate on continuous data streams and must produce timely, high-quality results despite executing on complex, failure-prone infrastructure. Hardware faults, network disruptions, and performance anomalies caused by resource contention or system heterogeneity can severely degrade performance and violate real-time constraints. We focus on strengthening the resilience of the producer-consumer streaming pattern, a fundamental building block of scientific streaming workflows. We present two complementary techniques: (i) a dynamic, asynchronous, non-blocking checkpointing mechanism that preserves progress without interrupting computation, and (ii) a progress-aware load redistribution strategy that detects slow workers and proactively rebalances tasks. Together, these mechanisms maintain forward progress and balanced execution even in highly error-prone environments. Experimental results show that our approach reduces the impact of failures and performance anomalies by up to 6x, while introducing less than 1% overhead in failure-free execution.

</details>


### [188] [Towards Transparent Checkpointing with AI-driven Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30921)
*Hai Duc Nguyen,Tekin Bicer,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究表明，前沿大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7）能够在无人工干预的情况下，端到端地为MPI科学应用程序生成可靠的检查点/重启代码，性能与人工实现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为MPI科学应用程序添加可靠的检查点/重启支持是一项耗时且需要专业知识的任务，研究者希望探索前沿大语言模型能否在无人工干预的情况下端到端地完成这项工作。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖多领域和计算模式的MPI应用程序基准测试套件，通过OpenCode CLI调用Anthropic的Claude Opus 4.7模型，为每个应用程序驱动一个迭代的代码生成循环。

Result: 在六个科学应用程序中，LLM平均在50分钟内生成了可工作的检查点/重启代码，平均每个应用消耗340万tokens。生成的代码在五个应用中在正常运行时几乎没有开销，并能从注入的进程故障中恢复，效率与人工实现的检查点/重启相当。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，自动化、端到端的LLM驱动的弹性工程对于相当一部分HPC应用程序来说，在技术上已经可行。

Abstract: Adding reliable checkpoint/restart support to an MPI scientific application is a time-consuming expert effort that requires deep knowledge of both the application and resilience. We ask whether a frontier large language model can perform this work end-to-end without human intervention. We assemble a benchmark suite of MPI applications spanning diverse domains and computation patterns, and drive an iterative code-generation loop for each application using Anthropic's Claude Opus 4.7 invoked through the OpenCode CLI. Across six scientific applications, the LLM generates working checkpoint/restart code in 50 minutes on average while consuming 3.4 M tokens per application. The generated code adds negligible overhead during normal failure-free execution on five of six applications and recovers from injected process failures with efficiency comparable to human-engineered checkpoint/restart implementations. These results suggest that automated end-to-end LLM-driven resilience engineering is technically viable today for a meaningful fraction of HPC applications.

</details>


### [189] [Omni-Flow: A Unified Workflow Orchestration and Distributed KV Cache Sharing Framework for Multimodal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31093)
*Bin Xiao,Jingfu Dong,Changran Wang,Yitian Chen,Xiaoyu Zhao,Yuqi Peng,Jianping Lin,Yuchen Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Omni-Flow是一个面向多模态大模型推理的分布式调度框架，通过三层抽象（控制流、数据流、计算流）解决了多模态工作流编排复杂、中间数据传输效率低、KV缓存跨角色共享困难等问题，实现了异构计算单元的统一调度与资源优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理从纯文本演进到多模态范式，现有推理系统面临三大挑战：（1）异构计算单元间复杂的依赖关系与并发控制导致多模态工作流编排困难；（2）海量中间数据跨进程、跨节点传输效率低下；（3）跨角色KV缓存与模型权重共享困难，造成GPU内存冗余。现有方案将LLM与扩散模型独立部署，缺乏系统级抽象，导致编排逻辑分散、传输路径与特定模型紧耦合、新模型集成成本高。

Method: 提出Omni-Flow分布式调度框架，采用三层抽象设计：（1）控制流层——通过Python DSL定义工作流，将异构单元编排为统一数据流图，支持静态DAG与动态路由，内置服务发现与多种负载均衡策略；（2）数据流层——提供分布式KV缓存抽象（超越prefill/decode分离），统一分配机制，基于零拷贝、低延迟通道实现GPU/CPU/SSD三层分页存储层级上的跨角色直接传输；（3）计算流层——支持复杂多模态前缀匹配以实现多轮对话KV复用，通过统一SGLang接口接管KV缓存与采样逻辑，使扩散模型在统一并行语义下直接复用LLM前向路径。

Result: 实验证明Omni-Flow能够以一致的编程模型支持多种异构场景，包括全模态对话系统和复杂图像生成流程，有效解决了多模态推理中的编排、传输与资源共享问题。

Conclusion: Omni-Flow通过系统级三层抽象实现了多模态LLM推理的统一调度框架，有效解决了异构工作流编排、高效数据传输与跨角色资源共享等关键挑战，为多模态大模型推理系统的设计与实现提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) inference evolves from text-only to multimodal paradigms, inference systems face three challenges: (1) flexible orchestration of multimodal workflows, where heterogeneous computing units exhibit complex dependencies and concurrent control; (2) efficient transmission of massive intermediate data across processes and nodes, with tensors flowing at high speed among heterogeneous roles; and (3) efficient sharing of KV caches and model weights across roles to eliminate redundant GPU memory. Existing solutions deploy LLMs and diffusion models independently, lacking a system-level abstraction for multimodal pipelines; this scatters orchestration logic, tightly couples transmission paths to specific models, and incurs high cost to integrate new models. To address these challenges, we present Omni-Flow, a distributed scheduling framework for multimodal inference through a three-layer abstraction. The Control Flow layer defines workflows via a Python DSL, orchestrating heterogeneous units into a unified dataflow graph that supports static DAGs and dynamic routing, with built-in service discovery and diverse load-balancing strategies. The Data Flow layer provides a distributed KV cache abstraction beyond prefill/decode separation, unifying allocation and enabling direct cross-role transmission across a three-tier paged storage hierarchy (GPU/CPU/SSD) over zero-copy, low-latency channels. The Compute Flow layer supports complex multimodal prefix matching for KV reuse across multi-turn dialogues, and takes over KV cache and sampling logic via a unified SGLang interface, letting diffusion models directly reuse the LLM forward path under unified parallel semantics. We demonstrate that Omni-Flow supports diverse heterogeneous scenarios with a consistent programming model, including omni-modal dialogue (LongCat-Next) and complex image generation pipelines (HunyuanImage-3).

</details>


### [190] [AC$^2$P$^2$SL: Adaptive Communication-Computation Pipeline Parallel Split Learning over Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31276)
*Chenyu Liu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Zirui Chen,Zhaohui Yang,Chunhui Feng,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AC²P²SL框架，通过将通信与计算过程统一建模为流水线，实现细粒度流水线并行，显著减少分割学习在无线边缘网络中的训练延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分割学习（SL）在无线边缘网络中采用串行执行计算与通信的方式，导致训练时间过长。尽管SL能保护本地数据隐私并利用分布式资源，但其顺序执行特性成为性能瓶颈，亟需解决。

Method: 提出自适应通信-计算流水线并行分割学习（AC²P²SL）框架：1) 将UE和BS的通信与计算过程统一建模为流水线，在多个微批次间实现细粒度流水线并行，有效重叠通信与计算；2) 考虑通信、计算、存储约束及UE异构性，构建联合优化问题以最小化训练时间，并提出分割与预分配算法；3) 针对动态环境设计自适应重分配策略以增强系统韧性。

Result: 大量实验表明，AC²P²SL在确保数据隐私保护的前提下，能够有效且鲁棒地减少训练时间。所提框架通过流水线并行显著降低了整体训练延迟。

Conclusion: AC²P²SL框架成功克服了传统SL的顺序执行限制，通过流水线并行实现了通信与计算的有效重叠，为无线边缘网络中的隐私保护协作模型训练提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In wireless edge networks, split learning (SL) enables base station (BS) to utilize the distributed data and computing power across user equipments (UEs) to achieve collaborative model training while protecting local data privacy. However, the inherent sequential execution of computation and communication processes in conventional SL usually leads to long training times. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes an adaptive communication-computation pipeline parallel split learning (AC$^2$P$^2$SL) framework. By conceptualizing the communication and computation processes of UEs and the BS as a unified pipeline, AC$^2$P$^2$SL achieves fine-grained pipeline parallelism across multiple micro-batches. Through this approach, effective overlapping of communication and computation is achieved which results in significant reduction of the overall training latency. Moreover, by considering the system constraints in the communication, computation, and storage dimensions as well as the heterogeneity of UEs, we formulate a joint optimization problem to minimize the training time and propose a corresponding split and pre-allocation algorithm to further enhance the pipeline efficiency. Additionally, accounting for the practical dynamic environments for the UEs, we design an adaptive re-allocation strategy to enhance the system resilience. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of AC$^2$P$^2$SL in reducing training time while ensuring data privacy preservation.

</details>


### [191] [An Empirical Analysis of High-Performance Computing Education in Germany](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31300)
*Anna-Lena Roth,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究系统调研了德国102所高校的高性能计算(HPC)教育现状，发现虽然67.6%的机构提供HPC课程，但存在理论与实践能力培养的结构性失衡：课程多为硕士阶段选修课，且尽管61.8%的机构拥有HPC集群，仅23.0%明确用于教学，导致资源管理、并行调试等实践能力培养受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算(HPC)的重要性日益增长，需要将并行编程和性能导向能力系统性地整合到计算科学课程中。然而，目前缺乏跨机构证据来了解HPC教育的实施方式以及课程设置与本地基础设施之间的关系。研究者旨在通过实证评估填补这一空白。

Method: 研究采用系统性实证调研方法，分析德国102所学术机构的模块手册和课程目录，识别出178门HPC相关课程，评估其能力覆盖范围和课程位置。同时评估本地学术HPC集群基础设施的可用性、规模和教学可访问性文档。通过统计分析检验基础设施教学访问限制与课程实践能力培养之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现：67.6%的机构提供至少一门HPC课程，但主要集中在硕士阶段的选修模块，本科阶段整合有限。61.8%的机构运营HPC集群，但仅23.0%明确记录用于教育目的，基础设施主要保留给研究使用。统计分析显示，教学访问受限与资源管理、集群使用、并行调试和性能分析等实践能力培养的减弱存在显著关联。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了德国高等教育中理论教学与HPC实践能力发展之间的结构性失衡。尽管大多数机构提供HPC课程和基础设施，但基础设施主要服务于研究而非教学，限制了学生获得实践HPC能力的机会。这种错配影响了资源管理、并行调试和性能分析等关键实践技能的培养。

Abstract: The growing importance of High-Performance Computing (HPC) requires the systematic integration of parallel programming and performance-oriented competencies into computational science curricula. Effective HPC education combines theoretical foundations with practical experience on real cluster infrastructures, enabling students to understand scalability, efficiency, and architectural differences between shared and distributed memory systems. However, cross-institutional evidence on how HPC education is implemented, and how curricula relate to locally available infrastructure, remains limited. We address this gap through a systematic empirical assessment of HPC education at 102 academic institutions in Germany. Based on module handbooks and course catalogs, we identified 178 HPC-related courses and evaluated their competency coverage and curricular placement. We additionally assessed local academic HPC cluster infrastructures with respect to availability, size, and documented accessibility for teaching. The results show that 67.6% of institutions offer at least one HPC-related course, but these offerings are predominantly elective modules at the master's level, with limited integration in bachelor's programs. Although 61.8% of institutions operate HPC clusters, only 23.0% explicitly document their availability for educational use, as infrastructures are mainly reserved for research. Statistical analysis indicates a significant association between restricted teaching access and reduced curricular emphasis on practical competencies such as resource management, cluster usage, parallel debugging, and performance analysis. Overall, the findings reveal a structural imbalance between theoretical instruction and the development of practical HPC competencies in German higher education.

</details>


### [192] [Performance Analysis in Parallel Programming Education: A Comparative Usability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31458)
*Anna-Lena Roth,David James,Jonas Posner,Michael Kuhn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EduMPI，一个面向并行编程教育的MPI性能分析工具，通过直观的GUI和实时可视化降低学习门槛，用户研究证明其相比专业工具有更好的教育价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行编程教育需要学生在HPC环境中获得实践经验，但现有的专业性能分析工具复杂度高、需要深厚的集群架构和MPI知识，阻碍了其在教学中的应用，因此需要开发更适合教育的简化工具。

Method: 开发EduMPI学习支持工具，提供直观的GUI界面，自动在集群上执行程序，并提供MPI通信的近实时可视化，使学生能够追踪集群中进程的物理位置通信并交互式检测性能问题；通过用户研究将EduMPI与现有专业性能分析工具进行对比评估。

Result: 用户研究表明，EduMPI相比成熟的专业性能分析工具降低了学习门槛，帮助学生建立对并行程序性能的直观理解，提升了教育价值。

Conclusion: EduMPI成功解决了专业性能分析工具在教育集成中的障碍，为并行编程教育提供了更有效的学习和性能分析解决方案。

Abstract: Parallel programming curricula encompass not only the development of parallel code and algorithm design but also emphasize efficiency, optimization, and performance analysis. To equip students with the skills necessary for writing efficient parallel code using message passing with MPI, practical experience on HPC environments is essential. Performance analysis tools assist in identifying issues such as load imbalances or bottlenecks. Despite their use by experienced developers, these tools' complexity and required knowledge of cluster architectures, resource management, MPI, and common parallel issues hinder their educational integration. To address these barriers, we developed EduMPI, a learning support tool designed to simplify cluster usage and performance analysis for students. EduMPI offers an intuitive GUI that automates program execution on clusters and delivers near-real-time visualizations of MPI communication. This enables students to track process communication according to their physical placement within the cluster and detect performance problems interactively. This paper presents a user study comparing EduMPI with established professional performance analysis tools, demonstrating that EduMPI lowers entry barriers and fosters an intuitive understanding of parallel program performance, thereby enhancing its educational value.

</details>


### [193] [LASER: Load-Aware Serving with Early-Exit for Reasoning LLMs at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31580)
*Zhiqing Tang,Size Li,Hanshuai Cui,Zilan Huang,Jianxiong Guo,Tian Wang,Yuan Wu,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了LASER框架，一种负载感知的推理服务方法，通过自适应早退和预算预分配机制，在边缘设备上部署大型推理模型时，实现了延迟降低17-38%，SLO满足率提升3-6%，仅损失1%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1）通过扩展思维链生成实现高性能，但在边缘设备部署时，长CoT序列与受限资源之间存在冲突。现有基于置信度的早退方法采用固定阈值，仅从单请求视角优化，忽视了边缘服务中的多请求并发和负载波动问题。

Method: LASER采用两个互补设计：(1) 负载感知自适应退出阈值，基于实时系统负载在经验验证的稳健范围内调整置信度阈值；(2) 难度和负载感知的推理预算预分配，根据请求难度和系统容量分配计算资源。将问题建模为推理质量和服务延迟的联合优化问题。

Result: 在两个推理模型、四个基准测试和多种负载条件下的实验表明，LASER相比固定阈值基线方法，平均延迟降低17-38%，服务级目标(SLO)满足率提升3-6%，平均准确率损失仅为1%。

Conclusion: LASER成功解决了边缘设备上部署大型推理模型时资源约束与性能需求的矛盾，通过动态负载感知机制实现了推理质量与服务延迟的有效平衡，为边缘场景下的推理模型部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) such as DeepSeek-R1 have achieved strong performance through extended chain-of-thought (CoT) generation. However, deploying them on edge devices raises a conflict between long CoT sequences and constrained resources. Recent confidence-based early exit methods reduce CoT length for individual requests, yet they apply fixed thresholds from a single-request perspective, ignoring multi-request concurrency and load fluctuation in edge serving. To bridge this gap, we propose \underline{L}oad-\underline{A}ware \underline{S}erving with \underline{E}arly-exit for \underline{R}easoning (LASER). LASER couples two complementary designs: (1) a load-aware adaptive exit threshold that adjusts the confidence bar based on real-time system load within an empirically validated robust range, and (2) a difficulty- and load-aware reasoning budget pre-allocation that assigns compute resources by request difficulty and system capacity. We formulate the problem as a joint optimization of reasoning quality and service latency. Experiments on two reasoning models, four benchmarks, and diverse load conditions show that LASER reduces average latency by 17--38\% and improves service-level objective (SLO) satisfaction by 3--6\% over fixed-threshold baselines, at an average accuracy cost of only 1\%.

</details>


### [194] [Decoupling Trust in Byzantine CRDTs: Fine-grained Post-Compromise Handling without Breaking Causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31759)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度信任模型，通过解耦身份信任与内容信任，在保持因果一致性的前提下，实现了拜占庭环境下CRDT系统对后妥协场景的灵活处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CRDT方法假设参与者为良性，在拜占庭环境下通常依赖基于身份的过滤来确保收敛。然而，在后妥协场景中（先前正确的参与者变为恶意），追溯排除其更新可能破坏因果依赖并使后续计算失效，现有方法难以应对。

Method: 提出将基于身份的信任与基于内容的信任解耦，构建结合两个维度的细粒度信任模型。基于确定性重构机制，允许副本保留已接受的更新，同时根据来源身份（如公钥）和单个更新的语义进行选择性包含或排除，并可融入应用层策略进行信任决策。

Result: 该方法在拜占庭环境下保持因果一致性，实现了对先前已接受更新的保留，同时支持基于身份和更新语义的细粒度选择，能够灵活处理参与者从正确变为恶意的后妥协场景。

Conclusion: 所提方法为去中心化CRDT系统提供了稳健且灵活的拜占庭容错能力，在保证因果一致性的同时，实现了对拜占庭行为和故障行为的精确控制。

Abstract: Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) provide strong eventual consistency without coordination, but classical approaches assume benign participants. In Byzantine settings, convergence is typically enforced through agreement on update validity, often relying on identity-based filtering. However, such approaches struggle in post-compromise scenarios, where a previously correct participant becomes malicious: retroactive exclusion of its updates may break causal dependencies and invalidate subsequent computations. In this paper, we decouple identity-based trust from content-based trust and introduce a fine-grained trust model that combines both dimensions. Building on deterministic reconstruction, our approach allows replicas to preserve previously accepted updates while enabling selective inclusion or exclusion based on both the originating identity (e.g., public keys) and the semantics of individual updates. Trust decisions can incorporate application-level policies, enabling precise control over the impact of each update on the system state. Our approach preserves causal consistency and enables robust and flexible handling of both Byzantine and faulty behavior in decentralized CRDT systems.

</details>
